  **THE HEADLINE.** A published "this ladder is monotone" reproduces on its own tape **28.4%** of the time — the mean bootstrap stability over the 6 of 84 ladders where the binary fires (min 0.1040, max 0.6000). The *headline* stability 0.8958 is a **78/84-weighted average of 0.9428 for the 78 NO verdicts and 0.2840 for the 6 YES verdicts**, so a run quoting "the binary is stable" is quoting the stability of a verdict that is almost always NO. Against the four outcomes declared before any number: **(C) BINARY IS CONSERVATIVE ONLY** — 0.9364 of 102 ladder-level reversals are UNDER-READ (0 OVER-READ of 84 on G_DOWNSHARE and G_SPEARMAN, 1 on G_ENDSE). **NOT (B)**, and the bar is scored as declared and not re-cut: no graded statistic beats the binary by 0.20 on BOTH stability and replication, because the graded ones buy replication with stability (G_SPEARMAN 0.6429 at 0.6947 against the binary's 0.0714 at 0.8958).

  **THE NULL A READER WOULD ASSUME IS WRONG, BY A MEASURED MARGIN — G5b, REPORTED NOT GATED.** The null every graded statistic here actually uses is SELF-ORIENTED (each ladder scored in its own end-to-end direction, as every committed claim is written) and it centres on **0.5420 +/- 0.0005, not 0.500**: orienting by the endpoints conditions on them and buys 0.0420 of down-share free. **At 9 rungs the honest chance level is 4.34 of 8 steps, not 4 of 8.** G5 PASSES separately at a fixed direction (1.81e-04), so this is the statistic's property and not the machinery's. **G6: strict monotonicity is the exact 2/9! = 5.51e-06 ordering** — a 1-in-181,440 event, not a 1-in-2 one, which is why the binary fires 6 of 84 times at all.

  **THE CENSUS — 1,417 COMMITTED MONOTONICITY SENTENCES ACROSS 353 FILES.** Of the 187 NARROW claims only **0.0909 state a null**, 0.3690 use graded phrasing, 0.3102 state a rung set and 0.1551 name the hold they were measured at. Three claims in four are published as a bare binary with nothing to check them against.

  **HOW MANY REVERSE: 0.5238 to 0.8095 OF TRANSFERRED CLAIMS, AT EVERY CELL.** The dial that moves the answer is the **GRADED STATISTIC and not the CLAIM SET**: G_SPEARMAN reverses about half and G_DECSTEPS about four-fifths at every claim set, while NARROW -> WIDE moves the share by at most 0.10. **A re-score that does not name its graded statistic has published a number with a 0.29-wide free parameter in it.** Claim-level reversals are mostly OVER-READ (0.8017 of committed claims assert monotonicity while only 6 of 84 measured ladders are) and ladder-level ones are 0.9364 UNDER-READ; **both are true and they are different objects** — the record's claims are SELECTED and the 84 ladders are a CENSUS — and neither is quoted as the other. The PINNED arm (claim also names its hold) is **2 claims and reverses 2 of 2: a direction, not a measurement**, published as such.

  **THE TWO DIALS AND NO MORE (rule 4, and the queue names both):** `CLAIM SET` {C_RHOONLY, C_CONTROLLED, C_ALL} x `TAPE` {T_IID, T_BLOCK, T_FACTOR} = **9 cells, EVERY ONE PUBLISHED** in `.grid.csv`. PANEL {U56, B136, SMALL} is not a dial; the 88-book population (N x H x cadence plus an 8-rung gross ladder) is not a dial and every book is published; the six drivers, SEED (three at every controlled cell) and the four rule-8 choosers are not dials. Frozen at 1082/1094/1098/1102/1110/1149/1151/1159/1161/1164's construction.

  **WHAT A CONTROLLED TAPE MEANS HERE, SAID UP FRONT.** A cross-book rho is taken over books that differ in the dial AND in everything the dial drags with it — which names get picked, how correlated they are, which episode the drawdown lands in. The three synthetic tapes make every column EXCHANGEABLE by construction, so varying one dial leaves nothing else to move: T_IID (pooled mean and sd), T_BLOCK (a 63-day moving-block resample of the panel's own returns drawn INDEPENDENTLY per column, keeping each name's serial structure and marginal while destroying its identity) and T_FACTOR (one block-resampled common factor plus exchangeable idiosyncratic noise). The benchmark on a synthetic tape is built from the SYNTHETIC names, never the real SPY, which would smuggle the real tape's episode structure back in through the 4b legs.

  **GATES 9 of 9 PASS, and G5b is a correction this run found and did not hide.** G1/G2/G3 the committed incumbent anchor 0.155520 / 1.138079 / -0.191276. **G4 c\* reproduces idea 1151's committed value for the incumbent OUT OF 1151's OWN FILE: 117.369 against 117.368.** G5 the calibration premise, |MaxDD| non-decreasing in gross on the real U56 gross ladder, min step +1.138e-02. **G5b REPORTED NOT GATED: 1162's committed WEIGHT identity `W(g) == g*W(1)` does NOT carry to the compounded RETURN path when gross < 1 — max |r(0.75) - 2.5*r(0.30)| = 3.331e-03 — because the cash sleeve compounds differently at each gross.** The run's own first cut gated on that identity and FAILED; the gate was replaced with the claim that is actually true and the deviation published beside it. Filed as 1177. **G6 the T_IID book's Sharpe is column-order invariant (2.0958 vs 2.1343) — the tape really is exchangeable.** G7 `synth()` deterministic 0.00e+00. **G8 T_FACTOR's median pairwise column correlation is 0.3929 against T_IID's -0.0002 — the common factor is MEASURED, not assumed.** G9 the harvest's claim sets partition it, 153 = 138 + 15.

  **(A) THE HARVEST — 906 COMMITTED FILES, 117,866 SENTENCES.** **153 pair a DRIVER verb with a CORRELATION token; 15 (0.0980) also name a control and 138 (0.9020) rest on a cross-book rho ALONE** — 1171's population, exactly. By topic: D_GROSS 12 sentences (11 rho-only), D_TURN 10 (9), D_N_SH 10 (8), D_N 9 (8), D_CAD 4 (3), D_HOLD 1 (0). **124 of 153 touch none of the six price-native topics and are DECLARED OUT OF SCOPE for the test arm rather than quietly counted.**

  **(B) THE ANSWER, AND THE THREE THINGS WORTH READING. 40 of 54 (driver, panel, tape) cells are RESOLVABLE and 14 are DEGENERATE — a tied dial or outcome column — published as such and NEVER scored as 'does not reverse'.** Over the 40, **0 REVERSE**; the observed rho matches the record's asserted sign at 24 of 40; median attenuation |rho_obs|/|rho_ctl| 0.9532. **(1) 1164's reversal does NOT generalise:** five of six price-native drivers keep their sign on every tape construction, including the most adversarial. **(2) BUT THE MAGNITUDE IS NOT A MECHANISM, AND THAT IS WHERE THE RECORD OVER-READS. D_N_SH — the EDGE-ladder premise — shows only 0.202 of its controlled tape's relation (+0.1659 against +0.9134), and D_N 0.582 (-0.4980 against -0.9377).** On an exchangeable tape book size orders Sharpe and drawdown almost perfectly; on the real panel SELECTION cancels four-fifths of it, because the names a small book picks are the ones the composite says are best. **The sign of an n-ladder claim is a mechanism; its rho magnitude is a NET of mechanism against selection, and the record quotes it as strength.** Filed as 1176. **(3) THE QUEUE'S OWN CHEAPEST DRIVER CANNOT BE TESTED THIS WAY AT ALL. 1094's turnover -> c\* is DEGENERATE at 9 of 9 synthetic cells and on 2 of 3 real panels, for a STRUCTURAL reason: c\* is defined only on books that PASS 4b, no synthetic book ever does, and a column of tied zeros is not a measurement.** On U56 the real panel reproduces the claim cleanly at **-0.6154** against 1094's -0.65 and 1151's -0.6484, so the claim is NOT refuted — it is **UNFALSIFIABLE by a controlled tape**, a different and more useful verdict than either pass or fail, and **every 4b-conditional outcome in the record inherits it.** Filed as 1175. **TAPE CONSTRUCTION IS THE DIAL AND IT MATTERS:** T_FACTOR carries 8 of the 14 degenerate cells (a one-factor tape makes books too alike to rank) and its max cross-seed spread is 0.9874 against T_IID's 0.1538 and T_BLOCK's 0.2130 — **a one-factor tape is the WEAKEST of the three as an instrument, and it is the one the queue named.**

  **RULE 8 AND BOTH KEEP PATHS — 264 REAL BOOKS, ALL PUBLISHED, AND ACTING ON THE DRIVER CLAIM LOSES TO ITS OWN FALSIFICATION CONTROL.** Benchmarks: **U56 SPY 15.06% / 0.8814 / -33.72% (halves 0.9598/0.8170), OOS 15.15% / 0.8684; U56 RULES v2 (live) @10 bps 8.60% / 1.1980 / -12.05%, OOS 9.42% / 1.2714; B136 SPY 15.16% / 0.8861 / -33.72%, OOS 15.33% / 0.8767; B136 LIVE 7.98% / 1.0993 / -12.24%, OOS 7.88% / 1.1059; SMALL SPY 14.06% / 0.8581 / -33.72%, OOS 15.33% / 0.8767; SMALL LIVE 4.30% / 0.6637 / -13.89%, OOS 3.75% / 0.5600.** Base rates at 10 bps: **4b full 19 of 264 (U56 16, B136 3, SMALL 0), 4b OOS 20, BOTH 19, 4a 0 of 264.** **CH_ANTI — deliberately picking the HIGHEST turnover inside the same IS-Sharpe band, the exact opposite of what the driver claim prescribes — BEATS CH_DRIVER on mean OOS Sharpe (0.8928 against 0.8202), on beating SPY (5 of 6 against 3 of 6) and on 4b full (1 of 6 against 0 of 6).** Tilting toward the driver costs 0.068 of OOS Sharpe on average, because low turnover on this tape means long holds and stale winners. **CH_DRIVER_CTL — act on a driver claim ONLY where a controlled tape RESOLVABLY AGREES — recovers CH_ISSHARPE EXACTLY at 6 of 6 picks, because D_TURN's control is degenerate everywhere and the rule refuses to act. That refusal is FREE and it saves the 0.068: an unresolvable control is not a green light, and this run's one transferable procedural result is that treating it as one would have cost real Sharpe.**

  **(A) THE ANSWER. `sign(BLOCK − IID) == sign(OBS − IID)` at 15 of 15 cells, BLOCK lies strictly between them at 14 of 15, `spearman(BLOCK/IID − 1, OBS/IID − 1) = 0.8286`.** The L ladder traces it end to end: at L=1 the block null EQUALS the iid null by construction, at **L=T it equals the OBSERVED value at 15 of 15 cells to 1.8e-13**, because one wrapped block is a rotation and a rotation splits an 18-to-155-bar episode in under 4% of draws. **THE FALSIFICATION LANDS: the published ordering REVERSES at 7 of 15 cells (5 of the 12 non-shuffle) — all three C_SPY cells, C_NOREBAL on U56 and B136, two of three C_SHUFFLE — and they are exactly the controls whose observed drawdown is DEEPER than their own iid null.** **C_SPY is the cleanest case in the run: BLOCK = STAT = OBS on every panel, phi exactly 1.000, cross-seed spread 0.00e+00, because SPY's worst drawdown is a 23-bar episode a 63-bar block carries intact in essentially every draw.** SMALL/C_NOREBAL does NOT reverse and that is the identity working rather than an exception (OBS/IID 0.7690 so BLK/IID stays at 0.9745). **H_MONO REFUTED AS DECLARED at 13 of 15 sign / 11 of 15 |rho|>=0.80 and NOT re-cut**; reported beside it, dropping the two C_SHUFFLE cells whose want-sign is a coin flip because their OBS−IID gap is noise, 12 of 12 and 11 of 12.

  **(B) ALL THREE CARRIERS THE QUEUE NAMED ARE DEAD.** **H_MEANREV REFUTED 0 of 3 and it is the decisive one: C_SPY is MORE negatively autocorrelated than the book and its ordering is the OPPOSITE SIGN on every panel (BLK/IID 1.0568 / 1.0471 / 1.0832 against 0.8807 / 0.8714 / 0.9400) — more mean reversion, opposite sign.** **H_GATE REFUTED 0 of 3**: removing the eligibility gate shrinks |OBS/IID − 1| on U56 (0.1526->0.1034) and B136 (0.1950->0.1610) but nowhere near halves it, and on SMALL it makes it LARGER (0.1101->0.2041) — the gate contributes, it does not carry. **H_CADENCE REFUTED 0 of 3 and goes the WRONG WAY on every panel** (0.1526->0.2597, 0.1950->0.3237, 0.1101->0.2310), flipping the sign on U56 and B136, because a never-rebalanced book concentrates into its winners and then takes a 72% / 60% drawdown over 120–143 bars. **H_VR REFUTED 9 of 15: VR(63) < 1 at 14 of 15 cells (0.458..1.126) so the long-horizon variance argument predicts BLOCK shallower EVERYWHERE, and the sign actually agrees at 9 — a |MaxDD| is an EPISODE statistic, not a variance functional, and averaging over a whole tape cannot see which single episode a block preserves.**

  **(C) THE CORRECTION TO THE PARENT, AND IT STARTS WITH THIS RUN'S OWN BAR BEING WRONG.** The pre-registered falsification H_SHUFFLE FAILED at 1 of 3 — **and the bar was wrong, not the measurement**: it demanded BLOCK == IID on a shuffled series, which the interpolation identity FORBIDS, since a shuffled path still HAS an observed drawdown differing from the iid median by sampling noise. **The verdict is recorded REFUTED as declared and is NOT re-cut.** The corrected 5-permutation sub-arm does not say what was expected either: **with order destroyed the block null is NOT centred on the iid null but sits systematically BELOW it — pooled mean BLOCK/IID 0.9696 over 15 permutations (U56 0.9804, B136 0.9789, SMALL 0.9495)**, a MACHINERY effect with a mechanism (a moving block is a contiguous slice of a fixed array and cannot contain the same bar twice, while an iid draw can stack the tape's worst days on top of each other). Since the shuffled series carries C_BOOK's exact marginal distribution, 1159's effect decomposes: **U56 total −0.1193 = machinery −0.0196 + ORDER −0.0996 (16.5%); B136 −0.1286 = −0.0211 + −0.1076 (16.4%); SMALL −0.0600 = −0.0505 + −0.0095 (84.1%).** **1159's ordering is REAL on U56 and B136 and MOSTLY AN ARTEFACT OF THE BOOTSTRAP on SMALL** — a correction no re-run of 1159's own grid could find, because its grid never contained a series with the order taken out. **H_EPISODE, declared POST HOC after Arm A and flagged as exploratory wherever quoted, REFUTED at 7 of 12 (bar 8 of 12, spearman(L*, episode) 0.5547); what survives is that L* >= the episode length at 12 of 12 non-shuffle cells with L*/episode 1.09..21.91 — the episode's length is a LOWER BOUND on L*, not a predictor of it.** VINTAGE: sign of (BLOCK − IID) unchanged at 4 of 5 controls, max |delta BLOCK/IID| 0.0357, the one flip being C_SHUFFLE whose sign is noise by construction.

  **RULE 8 AND BOTH KEEP PATHS — 180 books, ALL PUBLISHED, and 0 of 9 picks clear 4b.** Benchmarks: **U56 SPY 15.06% / 0.8814 / −33.72% (halves 0.9598/0.8170), OOS 15.15% / 0.8684; U56 RULES v2 (live) 8.60% / 1.1980 / −12.05%, OOS 9.42% / 1.2714; B136 SPY 15.16% / 0.8861 / −33.72%, OOS 15.33% / 0.8767; B136 RULES v2 7.98% / 1.0993 / −12.24%, OOS 7.88% / 1.1059; SMALL SPY 14.06% / 0.8581 / −33.72%, OOS 15.33% / 0.8767; SMALL RULES v2 4.30% / 0.6637 / −13.89%, OOS 3.75% / 0.5600.** Base rates **4b full 19, 4b OOS 21, 4a 2 of 180** (U56 10/12/0, B136 9/9/0, SMALL 0/0/2; both 4a passes are de-grossed SPY on SMALL, which is a statement about the live rules on a small-cap panel and not a proposal). **Three IS-only choosers on 2009–2016 pick C_GATEOFF 0.95 / C_NOREBAL 1.00 / C_GATEOFF 0.30 (U56), C_BOOK 1.00/1.00/0.30 (B136), C_SPY 1.00/1.00/0.30 (SMALL): 0 of 9 clear 4b full, 0 of 9 clear 4b OOS, 1 of 9 clears 4a.** **The best book clearing 4b FULL and 4b OOS is U56 / C_BOOK / gross 0.75 — full 15.55% / 1.1381 / −19.13% (halves 1.2049/1.0932), OOS 16.92% / 1.1615 / −19.13% — which IS THE INCUMBENT DEFAULT and NOT a discovery (1159 committed the same cell at the same numbers), and is unreachable by any chooser here.** The one non-incumbent 4b band is **U56 C_GATEOFF gross 0.55–0.75 (OOS 1.1461) and B136 C_GATEOFF 0.50–0.65**; a **MEMO IS WRITTEN with exact RULES wording and RECOMMENDS PARK** — the band is strictly dominated by the incumbent on Sharpe, CAGR and drawdown, rule 8 does not reach it, 4a is 0 of 180, and C_GATEOFF was built as a MECHANISM CONTROL, not proposed as a book. **NOTHING ENACTED.**

The bias does NOT cancel out of the 4b legs. It very largely DOES cancel out of every headline in (A)–(C), which are all ratios of one construction against itself on the same tape: BLOCK/IID, OBS/IID, phi, the L ladder and the machinery decomposition.

  **THE TWO DIALS AND NO MORE (PROTOCOL rule 4, and the queue names both):** `WINDOW` {W504, W756, W1260, WEXP} x `CADENCE` {R6M, R1Y, R2Y} = **12 cells per panel, 24 in all, EVERY ONE PUBLISHED** in `.cells.csv`. The gross RUNG is NOT a third parameter — it is the object the chooser SELECTS (1154's framing, kept verbatim) and all 33 rungs of both panels are published in `.grid.csv` whatever any chooser did. PANEL {U56, B136} is not a dial. Frozen at 1082/1094/1098/1102/1108/1110/1116/1117/1118/1150/1154's construction: CAND20 legs, cap INF, max_vol 0.60, min hold 126, N=20, cadence W, 10 bps (rule 2), LAG 1, warm-up 260, zero cash, block L=63, q=0.90.

  **THE COMMON SPAN, DECLARED BEFORE ANY NUMBER AND THE REASON FOR IT.** A rolling chooser cannot choose until warm-up plus one window of bars exists, so the four windows would otherwise be scored on four different tapes and could not be compared. Every cell, control and benchmark is scored from bar `WARMUP + max(window) = 1520` (**2014-01-21 -> 2026-09-15, 3,186 bars**), and WEXP is given the same first choice date, so the windows differ in LOOKBACK only and never in scored tape. **These figures are therefore NOT comparable to 1154's full-tape ones** (which start at bar 260); 1154's own numbers are replayed on 1154's own span in G8 and quoted from its file, never re-derived.

  **GATES 10 of 10 PASS, printed before any result number.** G1 fast runner == `engine.backtest` 1.39e-17; G2 committed U56 W/H126/N=20 triple 4.12e-05 PINNED at 2026-09-15 (15.5793% / 1.1397 / -19.1276%), **and the same gate UNPINNED reads 1.62e-03, 39.4x, from ONE extra bar of 4,706 — idea 1160's defect carried and published, not absorbed, with 1163 open on it**; G3 SPY OOS 1.70e-04; G4 1098/1102's n=12 triple 4.71e-05; G5 live RULES v2 MaxDD 4.95e-05; G6 determinism 0.00e+00; G7 `cadence_mask` == `engine.rebalance_mask` 0 bars; **G8 is the gate that licenses the run — 1154's one-shot C_IS4B picks (0.625 U56 / 0.575 B136) replay EXACTLY on 1154's own IS window and span, 0 of 2 mismatches, before anything new is read**; G9 the time-varying-gross build with a CONSTANT vector == the scalar path at 0.00e+00, so the splice machinery adds nothing; G10 the ladder nests the record's committed rungs. HYPOTHESES 4 of 5 SUPPORTED.

  **(Q1) THE ANSWER. 361 of 368 choices (0.9810) are INTERIOR rungs; U56 picks interior at 184 of 184.** All **7** endpoint picks are gross 1.000, all on **B136**, all on the two SHORTEST windows (W504/W756), and all clustered in **2021-07 .. 2023-07** — a 2-3 year lookback ending in that stretch contains no drawdown deep enough to make `M_DD` bind, so the tent degenerates into a monotone ramp and 1154's own identity (argmax-of-monotone is an endpoint) takes over. **The tent is structural to `min(M_S, M_DD, M_CAGR)`, not to the 2009-2016 window.** Pick levels median **0.5625 (U56) / 0.5125 (B136)**, range 0.300-1.000: the rolling chooser systematically DE-GROSSES against the incumbent 0.750 and sits below 1154's one-shot 0.625/0.575.

  **(Q2) IT MOVES CONSTANTLY AND MOVING IS ALMOST FREE.** Median move rate **0.8333**, range 0.360 (WEXP/R6M) to **1.000 — at the 2-year cadence EVERY re-choice moves the pick on both panels**; mean absolute step 0.0685 of gross, about 2.7 rungs. That is expected rather than a defect: a tent peaks where two noisy legs CROSS, the least resolvable point on a ladder whose whole-span Sharpe spread is **0.0041 on U56 (1.1303..1.1344) and 0.0082 on B136**. **The COST is the surprise — against the same cell with its gross frozen at its own median pick (paired, so the tape cancels), the moving adds a median of 0.118x NAV/yr of turnover = 1.18 bps/yr of drag**, range -0.12 to +6.04 bps/yr, the negative cells trading LESS than their frozen comparand. Switching gross trades only the CASH leg, so instability is cheap here in a way it would not be on a selection dial.

  **(Q3) RULE 8 AND BOTH KEEP PATHS. H_OOSPASS REFUTED: the 4b OOS pass SURVIVES re-choosing and beats the ladder's own base rate — 19 of 24 cells against 22 of 66 rungs (0.79 vs 0.33)**; 4b full 10 of 24 against 13 of 66; **4a 0 of 24 and 0 of 66**. Rule 8 was run on the PAIR: the (window, cadence) cell chosen on the FIRST HALF of the common span by **three independent honest choosers (C_H1SHARPE, C_H1CAGR, C_H1TENT) — all three picked a rolling cell and all three clear 4b on the untouched SECOND half, where the frozen incumbent gross 0.750 FAILS.** U56 rule-8 pick (W1260, R2Y): second half **14.73% / 1.2932 / -13.28%** against SPY **18.22% / 1.0746 / -24.50%**; full common span 11.89% / 1.1312 / -13.28% (halves 0.9507/1.2932), post-2016 12.55% / 1.1801. Benchmarks on the common span: **U56 SPY 13.74% / 0.8368 / -33.72% (halves 0.6054/1.0746), OOS 15.21% / 0.8711; U56 RULES v2 (live) 8.46% / 1.1960 / -12.05%, OOS 9.46% / 1.2763; B136 SPY 13.83% / 0.8413 / -33.72%, OOS 15.33% / 0.8767; B136 RULES v2 7.19% / 1.0342 / -12.24%, OOS 7.88% / 1.1059.**

  **AND THE HONEST READING IS UNCOMFORTABLE, STATED RATHER THAN SMOOTHED.** On that same second half **the frozen incumbent posts a HIGHER Sharpe (1.3332 vs 1.2932) and a much higher CAGR (19.65% vs 14.73%)**; it fails 4b **only** on drawdown, -15.77% against the -14.70% cap the rolling rule clears at -13.28%. **The rolling rule wins the 4b verdict by being SMALLER, not by being better.** 1150's identity re-measured on this span: across the whole 33-rung U56 ladder Sharpe spans **0.0041** while CAGR spans **16.97 pp** (4.16% -> 21.13%) — the gross dial is one book at different exposures, so a 4b verdict on it is decided by the drawdown cap and the CAGR floor and never by Sharpe.

  **B136 IS A FLAT KILL and that is half the case against enacting: 0 of 12 cells clear 4b full, and all three rule-8 choosers fail OOS on the Sharpe leg (1.0329 against SPY's 1.0637), the incumbent included at 1.0385.** A rule that is a KEEP on one current-constituent panel and a KILL on the other is not a rule worth real capital.

  **THE MEMO (written because 4b passed; PARK, NOT enacted — rule 6).** Exact RULES wording published in `.memo.md` as a *selector*, not a new book: *"on the first rebalance of each even calendar year, over the trailing 1,260 trading days compute M_S, M_DD and M_CAGR for each gross 0.200..1.000 step 0.025 and set the book's gross to the g maximising their minimum."* Four reasons not to enact: it adds no Sharpe (the dial cannot), the walk-forward's own row says the incumbent is better on Sharpe and CAGR, it fails outright on B136, and the chooser is unstable by construction. Honest discount stated in the memo: the chooser maximises trailing versions of the very legs 4b later tests, so the full-span pass is partly tautological; the second-half pass is not.

  **SURVIVORSHIP (rule 9).** U56 and B136 are CURRENT-CONSTITUENT panels, so every CAGR and drawdown LEVEL is optimistic and both the 4b drawdown cap and the CAGR floor are measured against an inflated book; the bias does NOT cancel out of the 4b legs. It very largely DOES cancel out of this run's headline claims — interior share, move rate and switch cost all contrast the same book against itself across windows on the same tape.

- 2026-09-17 (lane B, idea 1159 does-ANY-committed-MAXIMUM-KEYED-claim-in-the-record-carry-a-RESAMPLE-NULL) — **ANSWERED = ALMOST NONE DOES, AND WHEN THE NULL IS BUILT THE PUBLISHED NUMBER IS ARITHMETIC AT 35 of 36 CELLS: 1157's single SMALL/MAXDD result generalises to every maximum-keyed object, every ladder and every panel.** Two declared hypotheses REFUTED, one REVERSED at 12 of 12. A draft PROTOCOL clause priced ENACT-WORTHY on its own pre-declared rule (proposed only). A bycatch that bears on every cross-run gate in the record. No RULES change, no book promoted, no PROTOCOL edit (rule 6); RULES.md, PROTOCOL.md, engine.py, scan.py, bot.py and baseline.py untouched. SELECTION: this lane takes the LAST open idea; 1159 ended '## Open' and is not EDGAR / Form 4 / 8-K / options / live-data. Price leg of 81 rung books and 36 rule-8 picks, so it carries this run's mandatory rule-8 walk-forward and both KEEP paths.

  **THE TWO DIALS AND NO MORE (PROTOCOL rule 4, and the queue names both):** `CLAIM SET` {CS_MAXDD, CS_SPREAD, CS_ARGMAX, CS_ALL} x `NULL TYPE` {N_BLOCK, N_IID, N_STAT} = **12 combinations, ALL published** in `.dialgrid.csv`. PANEL {U56, B136, SMALL}, LADDER {N, H, GROSS, CADENCE} and RUNG are NOT dials — all 81 books built and committed at every point. PROXIMITY WINDOW {200, 400, whole unit} is not a dial (all three printed everywhere, nothing selected on any); q is not a dial (0.90 headline, 0.80/0.95 beside); BLOCK LENGTH frozen at the record's L=63. Everything else frozen at 1082/1094/1098/1102/1108/1110/1116/1117/1118/1122/1131/1140/1148's construction.

  **THE DESIGN.** A maximum's expected value grows with the count it is taken over — over BARS (1140's b(level) +0.2645, the only one of six statistics with a material one) and over RUNGS (1148's count inflation) — so three OBJECTS are built to match what the record actually publishes: **OBJ_MAXDD** (a |MaxDD| level), **OBJ_SPREAD** (max-minus-min of |MaxDD| across a ladder), **OBJ_ARGMAX** (best rung minus second-best). The SAME resampled index is applied to EVERY rung, so the tape cancels in the spread and margin (1012/1150's paired currency).

  **GATES 10 of 14 PASS, printed before any result number, and the 4 failures are this run's most consequential finding.** G1 fast runner == `engine.backtest` 1.39e-17; G4 live RULES v2 MaxDD == -12.05% 4.95e-05; G5 SMALL determinism 0.00e+00; G6 seed determinism 0.00e+00; G7 N_IID run-length share 2.5e-03; G8 N_STAT mean block 63 +/- 2.6. HYPOTHESES 3 of 5 SUPPORTED.

  **THE CENSUS — the idea's literal question.** Over **30,879 committed prose units** (LEADERBOARD rows, CHANGELOG paragraphs, 892 `*.result.md` files) there are **9,338 VALUED maximum-keyed figures**. Carrying a resample null: **228 (0.024) within 200 characters, 318 (0.034) within 400, 421 (0.045) anywhere in the same unit** — the most generous reading available; even the bare word "null" reaches only 557 (0.060). By claim set: CS_MAXDD 4,698 (0.021 / 0.038), CS_SPREAD 3,102 (0.027 / 0.050), CS_ARGMAX 1,538 (0.030 / 0.059). Machine-readable side: of **5,571 committed CSVs, 1,495 carry a maximum-keyed column and 267 of those (0.179) also carry a null column**. **"Does ANY" is literally yes and materially no: 95.5% of the record's committed maximum-keyed figures are published bare.**

  **THE RE-SCORING, AND IT IS EMPHATIC. Under the record's own moving-block null 35 of 36 maximum-keyed cells sit INSIDE their own 90% band; at q=0.95 it is 108 of 108 across all three nulls** (N_BLOCK 0.972, N_IID 0.944, N_STAT 0.972 at q=0.90). The single escape is reported rather than smoothed and goes the OTHER way: **SMALL / H / OBJ_SPREAD, observed 2.481 against a null median of 7.948 and a band [2.636, 18.904], with 0.952 of draws ABOVE it** — the one distinguishable maximum-keyed number on this grid is distinguishable for being too SMALL.

  **A SYSTEMATIC DIRECTION NOBODY HAS PRICED, and it is object-specific.** The observed value sits BELOW its own null's median at **89 of 108 cells (0.824)** (median observed/null ratio **0.936 / 0.851 / 0.964** by null type; median share-of-draws at or above the observed value 0.665 / 0.694 / 0.624 by object). It is **not universal and the exceptions are structured: OBJ_MAXDD is below at 36 of 36, OBJ_SPREAD at 26 of 36, OBJ_ARGMAX at 27 of 36** — all 19 exceptions are SPREAD or MARGIN objects, 12 of them on the H ladder. Resampling reliably yields DEEPER drawdowns than the record realised, and wider rung spreads / larger argmax margins only usually. Stated as measured, not explained; filed as 1161, because a null biased in a known direction is not yet a bar.

  **KILL 1 — H_SPREAD, and it reverses.** Declared: a max-minus-min over rungs of ONE tape would be the LEAST distinguishable object. Measured: OBJ_SPREAD is the **MOST** distinguishable (inside-band 0.917 against 1.000 for both OBJ_MAXDD and OBJ_ARGMAX), and its one escape is the low-side SMALL/H cell above.

  **KILL 2 — H_NULLDEP, reversed at 12 of 12.** Declared: N_IID destroys the autocorrelation that deepens drawdowns, so its |MaxDD| null would sit LOWER. Measured: **N_IID's |MaxDD| null median is HIGHER — 25.719 against N_BLOCK's 22.538 and N_STAT's 21.531 — at 12 of 12 cells**, so destroying serial structure makes simulated drawdowns WORSE and these books' realised return ORDER is drawdown-protective. Filed as 1162. **The verdict is nonetheless dial-INVARIANT where it matters: the inside-band share moves only 0.944 -> 0.972 across all three nulls and is 1.000 at q=0.95 under every one — the null choice moves the null's LEVEL materially and the published VERDICT barely at all.**

  **BYCATCH, AND IT MAY MATTER MORE THAN THE HEADLINE: THE RECORD'S COMMITTED CONSTANTS ARE NOT REPRODUCIBLE FROM THE REPO.** G2, G3 and G9 fail against 1148's committed numbers one day old. Decomposed rather than tuned away: **B136 and SMALL reproduce 1148's committed grid at 2.22e-16 and 9.71e-17 on the current tape**, so the pipeline in this file IS the record's and the whole deviation is U56's — the only panel whose cache advanced. Truncating U56 to 1148's own 4,705-row tape makes **G2V and G3V PASS, accounting for 97.3% of the deviation with one extra bar**. **The surviving 9.995e-05 residual is not a bar: `data/prices.csv` is NOT append-only.** Commit `6f1fcb1` ("Daily close 2026-09-16") rewrote the whole file — of **258,733 price cells shared by the two vintages, 31,505 (0.1218) were RESTATED across 48 of 58 tickers back to 2008-01-02**, max relative move **0.008384 (ITB)**, median restated move 1.50e-06. Every committed constant in this record is pinned to a price vintage that no longer exists on disk, and no truncation recovers it. Filed as 1163; reported here, not repaired. **No tolerance was widened and no committed constant was re-derived to fit.**

  **AND THE VINTAGE ARM SAYS SOMETHING ABOUT MAXIMA SPECIFICALLY, opposite to the naive reading.** The extra bar moves the same books' CAGR and Sharpe at 1e-03 and moves **ALL TWELVE maximum-keyed objects by EXACTLY 0.0000**: a new bar cannot touch a maximum unless it sets a new extreme, and U56's max drawdown is a 2020 event. This does NOT contradict 1140's b(level) +0.2645 — **a maximum grows with tape length in EXPECTATION, in jumps, and is flat between them**, which is precisely why watching it fail to move tells you nothing about its sampling distribution, and why it needs a null when a Sharpe does not.

  **THE DRAFT CLAUSE, PRICED (proposed, NOT enacted — rule 6).** *PROTOCOL rule 11, "no bare maximum": a backtest may not publish a number that is a maximum over a varying number of points — a MaxDD, a max-minus-min spread or band, an argmax margin, a worst-rung figure — without a resample null beside it: the null's median, its band at the declared q, and the share of draws on the published side.* Decision rule, declared before any number: enact-worthy only if compliance is near zero AND the null moves verdicts. **(a) compliance 0.045 of 9,338 figures at the most generous window; (b) 0.972 of re-scored cells sit inside their own band. Both fire: ENACT-WORTHY.** Cost is one column and one bootstrap; what it buys is that two runs' maxima become comparable at all.

  **RULE 8 AND BOTH KEEP PATHS — 81 books, 36 IS picks, NOTHING PROPOSED.** Books byte-identical across both dials, so this leg is invariant to both; run because rule 4 requires it. Benchmarks: **U56 SPY full 15.06% / 0.8814 / -33.72% (halves 0.9598/0.8170), OOS 15.15% / 0.8684 / -33.72%; U56 RULES v2 (live) full 8.60% / 1.1980 / -12.05%, OOS 9.42% / 1.2714. B136 SPY full 15.16% / 0.8861 / -33.72%, OOS 15.33% / 0.8767; B136 RULES v2 full 7.98% / 1.0993 / -12.24%, OOS 7.88% / 1.1059. SMALL SPY full 14.06% / 0.8581 / -33.72%, OOS 15.33% / 0.8767; SMALL RULES v2 full 4.30% / 0.6637 / -13.89%, OOS 3.75% / 0.5600.** Picks: **4b full 4, 4b OOS 4, 4a 0 of 36**; whole 81-rung grid **4b full 16, 4b OOS 17, 4a 0 of 81**. Median OOS Sharpe 0.9963, median regret against the frozen default **+0.0000**. **All four 4b-full passes among the picks ARE the frozen default rung** (U56 GROSS 0.75 and U56 CADENCE W, each picked by C_ISSHARPE and C_ISCAGR): OOS 16.92% / 1.1615 / -19.13% vs SPY OOS 0.8684. Best cell passing 4b full AND 4b OOS anywhere is **U56 N=12: full 17.65% / 1.1658 / -20.17% (halves 1.2741/1.0833), OOS 18.78% / 1.1701 / -20.17%** — **not a discovery** (1098/1102 committed the same cell at 0.1771 / 1.1692 / -0.2017 on their vintage) and **not reachable**, since no IS-only chooser picks it. **Nothing proposed, no memo, no RULES change.**

  **SURVIVORSHIP (rule 9).** U56, B136 and SMALL are CURRENT-CONSTITUENT panels, so every |MaxDD| LEVEL is optimistic and OBJ_MAXDD's observed value is biased shallow against a null built on the same inflated tape; the bias does NOT cancel out of a level comparison. It very largely DOES cancel out of OBJ_SPREAD and OBJ_ARGMAX, which contrast rungs of the SAME panel on the SAME resampled tape. 1157's committed SMALL/MAXDD band (0.441, 0.881) is quoted from its own file, never re-derived: this run measures a different object and is a SECOND construction bearing on the same question, not a reproduction.

- 2026-09-16 (lane C, idea 1150 is-the-CAGR-FLOOR-a-DE-GROSSING-DETECTOR-rather-than-a-COST-LEG) — **ANSWERED = YES, IT IS A DE-GROSSING DETECTOR AND NOT A COST LEG, AND THE QUEUE'S OWN WORDING OF THE HAND-OVER IS REFUTED: it is not SHARPE -> CAGR, it is L_DD -> L_CAGR, two EXPOSURE legs.** No RULES change, no book promoted, no PROTOCOL edit (rule 6); RULES.md, PROTOCOL.md, engine.py, scan.py, bot.py and baseline.py untouched. SELECTION: this lane takes the SECOND open idea; 1150 was second in '## Open' and is not EDGAR / Form 4 / 8-K / options / live-data. Pure price run — 66 books, 528 scored cells, 48 rule-8 picks — so it carries this run's mandatory rule-8 walk-forward and both KEEP paths.

  **THE TWO DIALS AND NO MORE (PROTOCOL rule 4, and the queue names both):** `GROSS RUNG` {0.200 .. 1.000 step 0.025, 33 rungs} x `COST RUNG` {0, 2, 5, 7.5, 10, 15, 25, 50 bps} = **264 per panel, 528 in all, EVERY ONE PUBLISHED** in `.grid.csv`. PANEL {U56, B136} is not a dial. **BENCHMARK VARIANT is not a dial either:** B_SPY is the PROTOCOL floor and the headline; B_SPYCOST (SPY charged the same rate on its own turnover) and B_SPYGROSS (SPY held at the SAME gross, rest in cash) are CONTROLS computed at all 528 cells and never selected on. The fine ladder NESTS the record's committed 0.30..0.75 step-0.05 rungs (G10). Frozen at 1082/1094/1098/1102/1108/1110/1116/1117/1118's construction: CAND20 legs, cap INF, max_vol 0.60, min hold 126, N=20, W, 10 bps, LAG 1, warm-up 260, IS end 2016-12-31, zero cash, block L=63, 1000 draws, crc32 seeds, q=0.90.

  **THE BINDING-LEG RULE, DECLARED BEFORE ANY NUMBER.** Each 4b leg re-expressed as the signed fraction of its OWN bar still in hand (Sharpe legs `(book-SPY)/|SPY|`; L_DD `(0.60|SPY_dd|-|dd|)/(0.60|SPY_dd|)`; L_CAGR `(CAGR-0.70*SPY_CAGR)/|0.70*SPY_CAGR|`), so **BINDING = argmin of the five**; `g*` = the highest gross rung whose binding leg is L_CAGR.

  **GATES 10 of 10 PASS, printed before any result number.** G1 fast runner == `engine.backtest` 1.39e-17; G2 committed U56 W/H126/N=20 triple 3.18e-07; G3 SPY OOS 1.70e-04; G4 1098/1102's U56 n=12 triple 4.97e-05; G5 live RULES v2 MaxDD 4.95e-05; G6 determinism 0.00e+00; G7 `cadence_mask` == `engine.rebalance_mask` on D/W/M/Q, 0 differing bars; **G9 the post-hoc cost transform `net = gross - turnover*c/1e4` == `engine.backtest(cost_bps=25)` at 6.94e-18**, which is what licenses pricing 8 cost rungs off one book per gross rung; **G8 reproduces the committed GROSS-ladder grid on all 20 rows x 6 statistics at 2.22e-16 with 0 of 100 leg-flag disagreements.** HYPOTHESES 5 of 5 SUPPORTED.

  **A CORRECTION TO THE QUEUE'S PREMISE, STATED FIRST.** The idea text says the four CAGR-killed cells are "U56 g 0.55/0.60/0.65, B136 g 0.50". Those are the record's first **PASSING** rungs. Every committed grid this tree can read, G8's comparand included, puts the L_CAGR failures at **U56 g <= 0.50 and B136 g <= 0.45** at 10 bps. The direction of the finding is unaffected; the run tests the boundary it can reproduce.

  **THE ANSWER. The hand-over EXISTS and is MONOTONE at 16 of 16 (panel, cost) points: g* = 0.600 on U56, 0.525 on B136** — below it L_CAGR binds, above it L_DD does.

  **IT IS NOT A COST OBJECT.** Moving cost 0 -> 50 bps, five times PROTOCOL's rung and the full width of the record's own ladder, moves g* by **exactly one rung of 0.025 on U56 (and only past 15 bps) and by ZERO rungs on B136**. On the paired block bootstrap (1012's basis — same 1,000 draws on both arms, so the tape cancels) median `g*(50) - g*(0)` is **+0.0250 U56 [90% -0.000, +0.050] and +0.0000 B136 [-0.025, +0.029]**, `|delta| <= 1 rung` in **89.8% / 92.9%** of draws, exactly zero in 35.2% / 44.1%.

  **THE MECHANISM, MEASURED.** At g* the book's CAGR sits **8.60 pp/yr (U56) and 10.44 pp/yr (B136)** below the same book at gross 1.00 with no costs, while 0 -> 10 bps of cost accounts for **0.263 pp and 0.257 pp**: cost is **3.0% and 2.4%** of the drop that puts the book through the floor. Across the WHOLE 0.20..1.00 ladder the full-sample Sharpe spread is **0.0016 to 0.0116** while the CAGR spread is **15.2 to 17.6 percentage points** — the queue's "1.1390 vs 1.1397" is the shape of the entire ladder, not a near-miss.

  **THE CONTROL THAT DECIDES IT. Under a GROSS-MATCHED floor — SPY held at the book's own gross, remainder in cash — L_CAGR binds at 0 of 528 cells and FAILS at 0 of 528.** Charging the benchmark instead (B_SPYCOST) changes g* at **0 of 16** points, because buy-and-hold SPY has no turnover to charge: **the floor's asymmetry is an EXPOSURE asymmetry, not a cost-rate asymmetry.** 1063's one-sided-handicap reading is the right object in the wrong currency.

  **KILL OF THE "SHARPE -> CAGR" WORDING.** Over all 528 cells a Sharpe-family leg binds at **47 (0.089)**; L_DD at 231, L_CAGR at 250, **L_OOS at 0**. On **U56 a Sharpe leg binds at 0 of 33 gross rungs at every cost rung from 0 to 25 bps**; one appears only at 50 bps. Consequence: the 4b pass set at 10 bps is a **CONTIGUOUS WINDOW between two opposed exposure legs — U56 gross 0.525..0.775 (11 rungs), B136 0.500..0.725 (10) — a band, not a peak**, and every rung inside it is the same book scaled.

  **THE LIMIT, MEASURED NOT ASSERTED.** g* as a NUMBER is barely resolvable: its unpaired 90% block-bootstrap interval spans **8 rungs on both panels** (U56 [0.450, 0.625], B136 [0.400, 0.575]). What IS resolvable is its INVARIANCE, because that comparison is paired and the tape cancels; every claim above is made in the paired currency. L_OOS is undefined on a resampled tape and is excluded from the bootstrap arm only (4 legs there, 5 in the point arm); stated, not hidden.

  **RULE 8 AND BOTH KEEP PATHS — NOTHING PROPOSED.** Only the 10 bps rung is a PROTOCOL-legal book (rule 2) — a book cannot choose its cost rate — so KEEP is asked there and nowhere else: **66 cells, 4b full 21, 4b OOS 22, 4a 0**; 4a is **0 of 528** at every rung and cost rung. Benchmarks: **U56 SPY full 15.10% / 0.8829 / -33.72% (halves 0.9588/0.8207), OOS 15.21% / 0.8711 / -33.72%; B136 SPY 15.16% / 0.8861 / -33.72%, OOS 15.33% / 0.8767 / -33.72%; live RULES v2 U56 8.62% / 1.2007 / -12.05% (halves 1.2322/1.1760, OOS 9.45% / 1.2762), B136 7.98% / 1.0993 / -12.24% (OOS 7.88% / 1.1059).** Best 10 bps cell passing 4b full AND OOS is **U56 gross 0.775: full 16.10% / 1.1398 / -19.72% (halves 1.2038 / 1.0972), OOS 17.54% / 1.1644 / -19.72%, 2.99x/yr** against the incumbent gross 0.75's full 15.58% / 1.1397 / -19.13%, OOS 16.97% / 1.1643 — **one rung away for +0.0001 of full Sharpe, inside every resolution floor the record has measured.** **Rule 8 is emphatic: 0 of 48 IS-only picks pass 4b full, 4b OOS or 4a** — C_ISSHARPE and C_ISCAGR both pick gross **1.00** (fails L_DD) and C_ISDD picks **0.20** (fails L_CAGR) on both panels, so the three honest choosers land on the two ENDPOINTS, i.e. on the two binding legs, and the passing band between them is unreachable by any of them.

  **SURVIVORSHIP (rule 9).** U56 and B136 are CURRENT-CONSTITUENT panels, so every CAGR and drawdown LEVEL is optimistic and the L_CAGR and L_DD bars are both measured against an inflated book; the bias does NOT cancel out of the 4b legs. It very largely DOES cancel out of this run's headline, which contrasts the same book against itself across cost rungs and against a de-grossed version of the same benchmark.

  **WHAT THE RECORD SHOULD DO WITH IT, STATED NARROWLY (proposed, NOT enacted — rule 6).** (1) A 4b verdict on a GROSS ladder is not a cost verdict: at PROTOCOL's rung cost moves the boundary by 0-1 rung of 0.025 and accounts for ~3% of the CAGR drop that causes the failure. (2) Any committed "this book dies on the CAGR floor" claim living on the gross ladder should carry the **gross-matched control**, which zeroes the leg at 528 of 528 cells. (3) "The binding leg hands over from Sharpe to CAGR" should be re-quoted as **L_DD -> L_CAGR**.

  Script `research/backtests/2026-09-16_is-the-CAGR-FLOOR-a-DE-GROSSING-DETECTOR-rather-than-a-COST-LEG_C.py`, 9 CSVs, console log, 6 LEADERBOARD rows. Follow-ups filed 1152 (is the 4b gross WINDOW's width a panel object or a tape object), 1153 (does any committed CAGR-floor death survive a gross-matched benchmark), 1154 (why do all three honest choosers land on a gross ENDPOINT).

- 2026-09-16 (lane B, idea 1140 is-DD-s-RUNG-ROBUST-UN-RESOLVABILITY-a-PATH-FUNCTIONAL-fact-or-a-TAPE-fact) — **ANSWERED = NEITHER. THE IDEA'S BINARY IS REFUTED ON BOTH HORNS AND A THIRD, NARROWER CARRIER IS NAMED AND MEASURED: it is MAX-ness, a strict SUBSET of path-functionality.** KILL of the path-functional reading, KILL of the pure tape reading, a surviving mechanism with a reproducing sign structure, a bycatch that bears on every committed H / CADENCE argmax, a measured LIMIT on the design, and a DEFECT in this run's OWN pre-registration reported rather than fixed. No RULES change, no book promoted, no PROTOCOL edit (rule 6); RULES.md, PROTOCOL.md, engine.py, scan.py, bot.py and baseline.py untouched. SELECTION: this lane takes the LAST open idea.

  **THE TWO DIALS AND NO MORE (PROTOCOL rule 4):** `STAT SET` {S_NONPATH, S_PATH, S_ALL} x `SUB-TAPE FRACTION` {1/1, 1/2, 1/3} = **9 combinations, ALL published**, and again at q = 0.80 / 0.95. PANEL (U56, B136), LADDER (N, H, GROSS, CADENCE) and RUNG SET (CORE, EXT) are NOT dials — all 5,184 cells are committed in `.cells.csv`. q is NOT a dial (0.90 headline, 0.80/0.95 beside, never selected on). BLOCK LENGTH is NOT a dial (L=63, frozen). Everything else frozen at 1082/1094/1098/1102/1108/1110/1116/1117/1118/1122/1131's construction.

  **THE DESIGN.** The idea's word is "path functional", so the test is matched: THREE PAIRS in which the only difference is whether the statistic reads the ORDER of the same return stream or only its MOMENTS — level CAGR/**MAXDD**, dispersion VOL/**ULCER**, ratio SHARPE/**CALMAR** — re-run over SIX DISJOINT sub-tapes of the warm record (1 whole, 2 halves, 3 thirds), i.e. 1131's own 16 blocks at three tape lengths.

  **GATES 11 of 11 PASS**, printed before any result number. G9 reproduces 1131's committed 16-block table on all 12 shared blocks at 0.00e+00 (1131's S_FULL == SHARPE, CAGR == CAGR, DD == MAXDD, same seeds, same draws); G8 reproduces all 54 rows of 1110's committed CORE grid at 0.00e+00. HYPOTHESES 2 of 5 SUPPORTED.

  **KILL 1 — the PATH-FUNCTIONAL reading.** ROBUST counts over the 12 mover cells per statistic: CAGR **0/12** vs MAXDD **12/12**; VOL **0/12** vs ULCER **7/12**; SHARPE **6/12** vs CALMAR **9/12**. The pair ordering runs the path way in all three pairs, so path-functionality contributes — but if it were the carrier the path arm would be 12/12/12, and ULCER and CALMAR both ESCAPE on the U56 blocks. **MAXDD is the only one of the six that never escapes anywhere.**

  **KILL 2 — the pure TAPE reading.** MAXDD is resolvable on **13 of 60** shortened-sub-tape mover cells at EXT against **0 of 12** on the full tape, over 5 distinct (panel, ladder, sub-tape) combinations of 20 — so there ARE stretches of this record on which its rung gaps resolve. But at the same fractions CAGR is 57/60, VOL 57/60, SHARPE 50/60, ULCER 24/60 and CALMAR 35/60: MAXDD is the most robust of the six at EVERY fraction.

  **THE MECHANISM THAT SURVIVES — MAX-ness, not path-ness.** Fitting log(y) = a + b*log(n) over the six sub-tapes, **MAXDD is the ONLY statistic of six whose realised rung GAP and whose bootstrap SD BOTH GROW with the tape**: b(gap)/b(SD) **+0.1419 / +0.1654** pooled (**+0.2606 / +0.1654** on the movers) against CAGR −0.3495/−0.4675, VOL +0.0004/−0.3181, SHARPE −0.4231/−0.4482, **ULCER −0.1221/−0.1916 and CALMAR −0.1699/−0.5543 — both path functionals have FALLING SD, like the moments.** ULCER averages the same drawdown path and CALMAR divides by the same maximum; only the maximum itself grows without bound in T. Its own level does the same (median |MaxDD| 21.52 / 16.67 / 16.32 at 1/1 / 1/2 / 1/3, b(level) **+0.2645**, the only statistic with a material one), which re-derives 1110's +0.2093 vs −0.4540 in a second currency.

  **BYCATCH, and it may matter more than the headline.** On the mover ladders the NON-PATH statistics' realised rung gaps shrink at **n^−0.5841** while their sampling SD falls at only **n^−0.4400** (textbook −0.50), so the resolution ratio gets **WORSE** with a longer tape: b(ratio) **−0.1251** for the non-path arm against **+0.0327** for the path arm. A gap that converges to ~0 is a rung effect that IS ~0. **A committed argmax on an H or CADENCE ladder in CAGR / VOL / SHARPE is not under-powered and waiting on data — more tape will never resolve it.** Filed as 1147.

  **THE LIMIT, MEASURED NOT ASSERTED.** A sub-tape differs from the full tape in LENGTH *and* in REGIME. On the same published column the WITHIN-fraction spread (same length, different stretch = pure regime) against the BETWEEN-fraction move (length + regime) is CAGR **3.50x**, VOL **5.52x**, SHARPE **1.66x**, MAXDD **5.19x**, ULCER **61.5x**, CALMAR **9.07x**. Every exponent MAGNITUDE in the file is therefore an upper bound on precision; **only the SIGN structure is asserted**, and it reproduces across both cuts, all three seed bases and both panels. Filed as 1148.

  **A DEFECT IN THIS RUN'S OWN PRE-REGISTRATION, reported and NOT fixed.** H_EXPONENT's arm medians pooled all 16 cells per statistic, including the N and GROSS ladders whose rung sets cannot move and on which 1131's claim does not live. Restricted to the 8 mover cells — a re-cut of already-published columns, not a third dial — the comparison moves from (+0.1107 non-path vs +0.1293 path) to (**−0.1251** vs **+0.0327**). The pre-registered decision rule's mechanical output is **TAPE**; it is published as the rule's output and no further, because its TAPE clause fires on an arm-median difference (+0.0186) that is inside the regime noise measured above and that reverses on the mover-only re-cut. **The finding this run stands behind is the narrower one.**

  **RULE 8 AND BOTH KEEP PATHS — 74 books, 48 IS picks, NOTHING PROPOSED.** Books byte-identical to 1131's, so this leg is invariant to both dials; run because rule 4 requires it. U56 SPY full 15.10% / 0.8829 / −33.72% (halves 0.9588/0.8207), OOS 15.21% / 0.8711 / −33.72%; U56 RULES v2 (live) full 8.62% / 1.2007 / −12.05%, OOS 9.45% / 1.2762 / −12.05%. 48 IS picks: **4b full 6, 4b OOS 6, 4a 0**, median OOS Sharpe 1.0244, median regret +0.0228; whole 74-rung grid 4b full 17, 4b OOS 18, **4a 0 of 74**. Every 4b pass among the picks is the frozen default rung (U56 GROSS 0.75 / CADENCE W): full 15.58% / 1.1397 / −19.13%, halves 1.2037/1.0971, OOS 16.97% / **1.1643** / −19.13% vs SPY OOS 0.8711. Best pick by OOS Sharpe U56 H/CORE 252: OOS 17.31% / 1.1931 / −21.84%. **Every 4b pass is the incumbent default — nothing proposed, no memo, no RULES change.**

  **SURVIVORSHIP (rule 9).** U56 and B136 are CURRENT-CONSTITUENT panels, so every level is optimistic and every exponent is fitted on that inflated tape; the bootstrap prices SAMPLING error only and cannot correct the bias. **Cross-read, not recomputed: 1141 (cloud, same day) found DD's "un-resolvable everywhere" is a LARGE-PANEL fact — it fires less on SMALL.** Independent evidence on the same side as KILL 2; this run does not rebuild SMALL.

  **THE TWO RULERS, DEFINED BEFORE ANY NUMBER. TRANSFER** = the object's own imported floor m x 0.0145, which 877/871 measured by REDRAWING A PLACEBO ARM'S SEED and which carries **no confidence** — the defect under test. **OWN** = the destination cell's resolution at q, the half-width of the two-sided q interval of the PAIRED block-bootstrap pick-minus-runner-up difference on the IS window only, the window the chooser sees; with AGREEMENT (the share of draws in which the difference's sign survives) reported beside it. The substitution is not a change of units but a change of OBJECT: a seed floor prices how far a Sharpe moves when a placebo is redrawn, while a rule-8 pick margin is a deterministic function of one tape whose only sampling uncertainty is the TAPE's. Nothing in the record had priced that swap as a verdict.

At CS_ALL / q=0.90 / HALFWIDTH, 116 of 263 admissible transfers change verdict (0.4411) and the direction is 116 TRANSFER_LAX to 0 TRANSFER_STRICT — the imported floor NEVER calls unresolved what the tape can resolve, it only ever certifies what the tape cannot.** Conditional on the object asserting RESOLVED (D1, post-hoc and labelled as such, a re-cut of two already-published columns), **116 of 116 = 1.0000 are refused by the destination cell's own ruler, and the own ruler certifies 0 of 263 in total.** Non-GROSS own/0.0145 ratio runs **8.84x to 22.35x, median 12.78x** — 1100's committed 12.9x reproduces — and **median pick agreement is 0.574 with only 2 of 14 cells reaching q=0.90**: the typical published pick's sign survives a coin flip's worth of redraws.

  **THIS RUN'S OWN DECLARED STATISTIC WAS THE WRONG CUT, AND THE FAILURE IS RECORDED RATHER THAN RE-CUT INTO A PASS. H_WRONG_RULER (majority of admissible objects flip) is REFUTED at 0.4411**, and the bar was unreachable on this corpus BY CONSTRUCTION: an unconditional flip share cannot exceed the share of objects that assert RESOLVED at all, which is 116 of 263. **A census asking 'how many verdicts change' must condition on the verdicts that could change** — filed as the lesson, not smuggled into the headline. H_DIRECTION SUPPORTED 116-0; **H_Q_STABLE SUPPORTED, flip share span 0.0038 across all five q**, so the ANSWER is not a free parameter even though the own ruler's LEVEL is; **H_UNDECLARED SUPPORTED — 163 of 263 admissible transfers (0.6198) state no confidence anywhere beside the floor they apply.**

  **THE CLAUSE AS DRAFTED (stated, not enacted):** *PROPOSED PROTOCOL 4c — EVERY PUBLISHED ARGMAX CARRIES ITS OWN RESOLUTION. A result that names the best rung of a ladder shall quote, beside it and in the same sentence: (i) the GAP from the peak to the runner-up, in the statistic's own units; (ii) the ladder's RESOLUTION FLOOR at the declared confidence q; (iii) the ladder's RUNG COUNT and its end rungs; and (iv) the TIE SET at that q — every rung the bootstrap cannot separate from the peak. Where the tie set holds more than one rung, the result is published AS THE TIE SET and not as a rung. Where the tie set is the whole ladder, no argmax is published at all.*

H_STABLE REFUTED: the DELETE-count swing CORE -> EXT is 0 at B_QUOTE and 0 at B_DECIDE on EVERY claim set — perfectly rung-stable — but 9 of 32 at B_TIESET** (CS_ALL32 18 -> 9, CS_HCAD 15 -> 6), against the 12-of-32 swing (12 -> 0) that killed the ban, **and in the SAME direction: adding rungs makes it delete FEWER claims.** 1117 proposed this clause on the ground that "nothing in it depends on a whole-ladder flag". **B_TIESET's delete trigger — *the tie set spans the whole ladder* — IS a whole-ladder flag**, and it moves almost as much as the ban's did. **THE GENERAL STATEMENT, STRENGTHENED FROM 1117's: a clause is rung-stable iff every quantity in its TRIGGER is a property of the peak and its own pair, never of the ladder. QUOTING a whole-ladder quantity (the floor, the rung count) is free; TRIGGERING on one is not.**

  **THE MECHANISM, MEASURED RATHER THAN ASSERTED. The null median is a SIGNAL AMPLIFIER, and its own sampling error — the term the idea named — is real but small.** Per rung pair, Var(dEDGE) = Var(dBOOK) + Var(dNULLMED) - 2Cov: the null's variance share runs **+0.0615 / +0.0681 / +0.0724 on U56 and +0.1220 / +0.1200 / +0.1283 on B136** at L = 21 / 63 / 126. Its cost in noise is **+3.6% / +4.2%** in median sd(pair gap) (1.6486 vs 1.5910 pp; 1.6322 vs 1.5659). Its gain in signal is **1.47x / 1.80x** in ladder spread (4.370 -> 6.407 pp on U56, 3.594 -> 6.471 pp on B136), because the DD-matched null's OWN CAGR runs the other way across the ladder (U56 11.88 -> 10.88 at n=12 -> 13.04 pp at n=40; B136 13.19 -> 9.77 -> 13.46) while the book's falls monotonically. **Net signal-to-noise 1.42x on U56 and 1.73x on B136 in EDGE's favour.** H_FIXNULL_MID is the one SUPPORTED hypothesis (6 of 6) but is second-order and in one place runs backwards: holding the null median at its point value leaves REL_FLOOR **identical at 5 of 6** points and makes it **worse** at U56 L=126 (0.5077 -> 0.7273). Its single real gain is at the peak on B136, where EDGE_FIXNULL is **the only cell in the whole run that DECIDES** the nine-rung ladder (tie set {10} at all three block lengths).

Recorded separately: **the UNPAIRED reliability estimator is 0.0000 at all 48 cells on all three panels**; only the PAIRED form, the error variance that actually enters the sign agreement the floor is built from, recovers anything at all.

The floor is infinite only when NO resolved pair sits ABOVE the LARGEST un-resolved gap, so one well-separated pair at the top of a right-skewed gap distribution makes a cell finite however poor the panel's AVERAGE precision is — and SMALL663's gaps are the most right-skewed (mean max/median gap **3.36** vs 2.53 / 2.28) while having the SMALLEST share of pairs separated by more than 2 pair-SDs (0.0833 vs 0.1389 / 0.1965). **Stated as a caution, not a measured law: an INF_FLOOR count is NOT a panel-precision ranking and must not be read as one.** This run establishes only that it is not monotone in mean SNR across three panels.

CONFIRM of 1097's incident and its mechanism; KILL of "record-wide" as a description of the seed exposure; KILL of H_CAPONLY; CORRECTION to the implied scope. No RULES change, no book promoted, no PROTOCOL edit (rule 6); RULES.md, PROTOCOL.md, scan.py, bot.py and baseline.py untouched.** SELECTION: this lane takes the LAST open idea; 1105 was the last line under '## Open' and has a PRICE LEG — pricing what a re-read gets wrong means rebuilding 1071's capped books and both null families on the tape — so it carries this run's mandatory rule-8 walk-forward and both KEEP paths.

  **THE TWO DIALS AND NO MORE (PROTOCOL rule 4):** `COLUMN CLASS` {SEED_KEY, ANY_KEY} x `ROUND-TRIP TEST` {LITERAL, SEMANTIC}. **All 4 grid points published.** SEED_KEY = a column whose name is a token in one of the record's **44 committed `mdseed(...)` call sites**, restricted to tokens of >= 3 characters — the short ones (`s`, `t`, `k`, `n`, `p`, `c`, `f`, `r`, `l`) are LOOP VARIABLES, and leaving them in matched t-stats, p-values and counts, inflating the class with columns no reader ever keys on. **That restriction is part of the D1 definition, declared before the answer, and the first pass without it is what showed why it is needed.** LITERAL = `str(pd.read_csv(f)[col])` differs from the committed text; SEMANTIC = the raw -> re-read map is not injective, so no format rule can undo the damage. Panels, the `n` ladder and 1071's cap ladder are COORDINATE SETS.

  **THE CENSUS. 5,302 committed CSVs, 87,836 columns (this run's own outputs self-excluded).** SEED_KEY LITERAL **98 of 3,541 (2.77%) in 97 of 5,302 files (1.83%)**; SEED_KEY SEMANTIC 60 (1.69%); ANY_KEY LITERAL **45,644 of 87,836 (51.97%) in 88.66% of files**; ANY_KEY SEMANTIC 7,281 (8.29%). **H_WIDE FAILS at 1.83% against its 5%-of-files bar and H_SEED FAILS by a factor of 19 — seed-key columns round-trip BETTER than columns at large, not worse.** 1097's incident is real and reproduced, but it is not a record-wide seed defect.

  **THE MODE BREAKDOWN IS THE PART WORTH KEEPING.** Of the 98 SEED_KEY literal failures: **KEY 22 cols / 22,357 cells** (1071's kind — a literal whose FORMAT carries the key), **EMPTY_NAN 42 / 40,599** (a committed `''` returning as the string `'nan'`), **FLOAT_REPR 34 / 6,195** (17-sig-fig text vs pandas' 16-digit repr). Across ALL columns the ordering inverts completely: **FLOAT_REPR 39,430 cols / 18.2M cells**, EMPTY_NAN 6,137 / 2.88M, **KEY 99 / 76,467**. `CAGR` alone fails in 1,206 files and 1.39M cells — and that is harmless, because nobody hashes a CAGR. **The defect is not "CSV columns do not round-trip"; it is "a key must never be recovered from a float-inferred column", and only ~100 columns in the record are keys.**

  **H_CAPONLY FAILS, AND THAT IS THE FINDING WITH TEETH.** Ten seed-key names fail LITERAL: `cap`, `kind`, `panel`, `gate`, `seed`, `null`, `elig`, `native`, `u56`, `U56`. **`kind` (23 files, 38,656 cells, SEMANTICALLY lossy in 23 of 23) and `panel` (26 files, 23,532 cells, lossy in 25 of 26) are worse than `cap` (24 files, 5,880 cells, lossy in 1 of 24)**, and `seed` itself fails in 4 files, all four semantically. **60 SEED_KEY columns are semantically lossy: for those no re-reading convention recovers the committed key at all**, and they must be re-derived from their scripts, never from their CSVs.

  **THE PRICE. 24 cells (2 panels x n {5,10,20} x 1071's caps {INF, 2.00, 1.50, 1.00}, H=126, 20 seeds), all published.** Every one of the four committed cap literals fails to survive — pandas returns `inf`, `2.0`, `1.5`, `1.0`, each giving a different md5 seed, shown from pandas itself rather than asserted. Re-seeding the null families from the re-read literal moves the committed EDGE by **median 0.4013 pp, max 2.0898 pp** against a median seed SE of 0.3221 pp. **0 of 24 cells reproduce the committed EDGE exactly; 8 of 24 (33.3%) land inside one seed SE** — 1097's "8 of 40" reproduced on an independent grid and construction.

Only with G6 passing is a non-zero delta attributable to the key rather than to the machinery. **HYPOTHESES 2 of 5** — H_SEMANTIC and H_PRICE pass; H_WIDE, H_SEED and H_CAPONLY fail, and those three failures are the answer.

  **RULE 8 AND BOTH KEEP PATHS — nothing proposed.** IS 2009-2016 chooses, OOS 2017-2026 read once. **4a 0 of 24, 4b full 0 of 24, 4b OOS 7 of 24.** Binding leg `L_CAGR` **0 of 24**: the capped composite tops out at 10.41% full-sample CAGR against the 10.57% floor (70% of SPY's 15.10%) — a **0.16 pp** miss, well inside idea 1083's measured 4.1-7.2 pp resolution for exactly that quantity, so the miss is not resolvable either. `L_DD` 24/24, `L_H1` 20/24, `L_OOS` 19/24, `L_H2` 7/24. SPY U56 full 15.10% / 0.8829 / -33.72%, OOS 15.21% / 0.8711 / -33.72%; B136 full 15.16% / 0.8861 / -33.72%, OOS 15.33% / 0.8767 / -33.72%. Live RULES v2 8.62% / 1.2007 / -12.05%.

  **THE TWO DIALS AND NO MORE (PROTOCOL rule 4):** `H` (min hold, index positions) walked at **{5, 10, 21}** x `PANEL` **{U56, B136}**. On a WEEKLY cadence the rebalance dates are ~5 trading days apart, so these rungs are "the null re-draws every 1 / 2 / 4 rebalances"; **H=5 is the floor — nothing is ever young at the next rebalance, so the ordering is re-drawn in full every week.** 1086's committed **H {63, 126}** rungs are CARRIED AS CONTEXT and used as cross-run reproduction gates, not tuned. **THE N LADDER IS NOT A DIAL** — `{5,8,10,12,15,20,25,30,40}` is 1082/1086's committed coordinate set and every rung is published. **90 cells, 180 prices, all committed.** Frozen at 1082/1085/1086's construction: CAND20 legs, cap INF, REBUILT DD-match, 40 seeds, 34 bisection steps, max_vol 0.60, gross 0.75, W, 10 bps, LAG 1. **The null SEED RECIPE contains NO H**, so the SAME 40 random orderings are replayed at every rung: a move in GATE along H is the min hold moving, not a different draw.

  **THE ANSWER.** U56 mean GATE down the ladder 126 -> 63 -> 21 -> 10 -> 5 reads **-0.465 -> -0.872 -> +0.216 -> -0.050 -> +0.907 pp** while the null's realised turnover rises monotonically **2.70 -> 4.65 -> 11.10 -> 22.45 -> 43.76** turns/yr. **H_TURNOVER FAILS: `Spearman(GATE, NTURN_raw OPEN)` = +0.0775 pooled, +0.4924 on U56, -0.0572 on B136** — the two panels disagree in sign, which is the declared refutation. **H_MONOTONE FAILS with 2 inversions. H_FLIP_EXTENDS FAILS: positive-GATE rungs go 6/9 at H=21 -> 5/9 at H=10 -> 8/9 at H=5** — halving the hold, i.e. DOUBLING the re-draw rate, makes the flip WEAKER.

  **H_DTURN FAILS AT ITS OWN NAMED MECHANISM.** The premise is that the gate costs a random book a TURNOVER cost, so the gate-free null must be the one turning over more. `DTURN = NTURN(OPEN) - NTURN(ELIG)` is **positive at only 40 of 90 cells** (mean +0.755, range [-3.139, +9.356]), and it is **NEGATIVE at EVERY rung H >= 21 on BOTH panels** — there the GATED null turns over MORE, the exact reverse of the premise. It only turns positive at H = 5 and 10. `rho(GATE, DTURN)` +0.204 pooled.

  **H_REDRAW PASSES, AND THAT IS WHAT MAKES THE FAILURE CLEAN.** Names newly taken per rebalance: **15.35 / 3.68 / 0.68 at H = 5 / 21 / 126**, ratios **4.18x (5-over-21) and 22.7x (5-over-126)**, inside the declared [3, 5] band at all four panel x gate arms. The queue line's arithmetic — "a 21-day hold lets a random ordering re-draw four times as often" — is exactly right, measured, and moves by a factor of 22 across the ladder. **GATE moves 1.4 pp, non-monotonically, with the wrong cross-panel sign.**

  **D1 (POST-HOC, LABELLED, EXCLUDED FROM THE TALLY) — IT IS A PANEL FACT.** H_PANEL as DECLARED asks only whether B136 flips positive ANYWHERE; it does, at 4 cells, so it passes on its wording. The DIRECTIONAL contrast is the one that bites and it runs the wrong way: **B136's nulls re-draw MORE than U56's at every one of the 5 rungs, and B136's mean GATE is MORE NEGATIVE than U56's at every one of the 5 rungs.** Two panels matched on construction, cadence, cost, seeds and DD convention order themselves by COMPOSITION, not by turnover (positive cells over the walk rungs: U56 19/27, B136 3/27). **The declared wording was too weak to test the premise, and this is recorded as a lesson about the wording, not as a pre-registered result.**

  **D2 (POST-HOC, LABELLED) — 'TURNOVER' IS TWO AXES AND THEY PULL OPPOSITE WAYS.** The +0.4924 on U56 is ACROSS rungs (the H axis). WITHIN a rung, where the only turnover variation is the N axis, **`rho(GATE, NTURN)` is NEGATIVE at 9 of 10 (panel, H) slices, as low as -0.967.** More null turnover RAISES GATE when it comes from a shorter hold and LOWERS it when it comes from a smaller book; no single "GATE scales with re-draw frequency" claim can carry both signs. Best of eight explanators tried is the literal re-draw count `NEW_per_reb` (pooled +0.302, U56 +0.615, B136 +0.167) — still weak, still panel-inconsistent.

  **WHAT THE FLIP IS, AT THE LIMIT OF WHAT THIS RUN ESTABLISHES.** A U56 property that strengthens as the hold shortens, and NOT noise where it is largest: **7 of 9 U56 H=5 cells are positive at 2 SE with NONE decisively negative**, against B136's 0 positive-decisive and 9/9/9 negative-decisive at H = 10/21/63. So 1097's 13 exceptions are real and concentrated — they are just not explained by re-draw frequency.

  **GATES 13 of 13 PASS, printed before any result number.** G1/G1b/G1c fast runner, `gross_rescaler(1.0)` and its turnover path all == `engine.backtest` at 1.39e-17 / 0.00e+00; G2 936/1071/1082's committed W/H126 N=20 triple 3.18e-07; G3 SPY OOS triples 1.70e-04 / 4.05e-05; G4 live RULES v2 MaxDD == -12.05% / -12.24%; G8 every book at gross 0.75. **The suite's point is G5-G7: 1097's 54 committed `EDGE_OPEN`/`EDGE_ELIG` figures at 1.78e-15, its 54 book CAGR/MaxDD/turnover triples at 1.78e-15, and its headline (U56 H=21 carries 6 of 9 positive GATE rungs) at 0.00e+00.** The H=21/63/126 columns ARE 1097's numbers; H=5 and H=10 are the only new measurement.

  **A DEFECT THIS RUN FOUND IN ITS OWN FIRST PASS AND PAID FOR.** Turnover is **NOT** exactly `lam *` the `lam=1` turnover under the REBUILT DD-match: the cost term is `lam * |W - A/V(lam)|` and the cash sleeve inside `V` does not scale, so recovering raw turnover as `matched / lam` errs by **1.31e-02, 1.75% of peak rebalance turnover**. The first pass did exactly that and its declared linearity gate G1d duly FAILED. `NTURN_raw` now comes from a DIRECT `lam=1` evaluation, and G1d PUBLISHES the size of the error the shortcut would have carried instead of asserting a linearity that does not hold. The headline correlations moved in the fourth decimal (+0.0780 -> +0.0775), so no conclusion rested on it — **but it was invisible except to a gate declared before the numbers.** Filed as 1111.

  **RULE 8 AND BOTH KEEP PATHS.** Cell chosen on IS 2009-2016 ALONE by two choosers declared in advance, OOS 2017-2026 read ONCE. **H_WF PASSES 1 of 4, and it is not an EDGE chooser.** C_SHARPE on U56 picks **H=21 N=12: OOS 17.57% / 1.1426 / -19.48%, 4b OOS PASS** — 1082/1086/1097's already-committed and already-parked cell, reproduced at 1.78e-15, **not a new candidate and not re-proposed.** C_EDGE on U56 picks H=10 N=5 and both choosers on B136 pick H=63 N=5; all three FAIL on the drawdown leg, reproducing 1082's, 1084's and 1097's reading from a fourth direction. Benchmarks: U56 SPY full 15.10% / 0.8829 / -33.72% (halves 0.9588/0.8207), OOS 15.21% / 0.8711 / -33.72%; RULES v2 live full 8.62% / 1.2007 / -12.05%, OOS 9.45% / 1.2762 / -12.05%; B136 SPY OOS 15.33% / 0.8767 / -33.72%, RULES v2 OOS 7.88% / 1.1059 / -12.24%. **4a 0 of 90** (`A_DD` 0/90 — no cell clears the live book's -12.05%, a book fact). **4b 25 of 90 full-sample, 28 of 90 OOS, 25 of 90 both**; `L_CAGR` 90/90, `L_H1` 89/90, `L_OOS` 73/90, `L_H2` 66/90, **`L_DD` 28/90 and the SOLE failing leg at 40 of the 65 failures.**

  **THE NEW RUNGS ARE WHERE THE PASSES CONCENTRATED, AND THE H DIAL TRADES ONE 4b LEG FOR THE OTHER. 17 of the 25 passes are H=5 or H=10 cells (15 U56, 2 B136)** — books the record had not built. Drawdown headroom median **+2.08 pp at H=5** against +0.55 pp at H=21; CAGR headroom **+2.23 pp at H=5** against +4.55 pp at H=21. Removing the min hold entirely buys the widest drawdown margin in the grid (U56 H=5 N=40 sits **3.56 pp inside the cap** against the 0.01-1.45 pp the committed cells manage) and pays for it in CAGR, where that same cell clears the floor by **0.34 pp**. **Shortening the hold moves the binding leg from `L_DD` to `L_CAGR` without ever clearing both by a margin this tape can resolve. NOTHING IS PROPOSED AND NO MEMO IS WRITTEN:** best headroom anywhere is +3.56 pp on drawdown and +7.14 pp on CAGR against idea 1083's measured 4.1-7.2 pp 90% width for exactly that quantity, and the H=5 slice is not rule-8 reachable — the honest IS-Sharpe chooser picks H=21 and the IS-EDGE chooser picks H=10 N=5, which fails OOS.

  **SURVIVORSHIP (rule 9).** U56 and B136 are CURRENT-CONSTITUENT panels, so every level is optimistic and every 4a/4b count an UPPER bound. GATE, DTURN and the EDGE contrasts are within-pool over one tape and the bias very largely cancels out of them; it does NOT cancel out of the 4b legs, measured against SPY, a real index, so the 25 parked passes are flattered by it.

- 2026-09-16 (lane B, idea 1097 RE-READ-every-committed-EDGE-FIGURE-as-a-LOWER-BOUND) — **ANSWERED = MOSTLY, BUT NOT AS A UNIVERSAL CLAIM. KILL of "every committed EDGE figure UNDER-states rank skill", a CORRECTION of the envelope 1097's own queue text quotes, and a CORRECTION to 1071's +5.07 pp — the record's most-cited EDGE figure, where the lower-bound reading turns out to be a CONVENTION question rather than a tape question. No RULES change, no book promoted, no PROTOCOL edit (rule 6); RULES.md, PROTOCOL.md, scan.py, bot.py and baseline.py untouched.** SELECTION: lane B takes the LAST open idea; 1097 was the last line under '## Open' and, unlike most record-census ideas in the queue, has a PRICE LEG — re-pricing a committed EDGE figure means rebuilding its book and its null on the tape — so it carries this lane's mandatory rule-8 walk-forward and both KEEP paths. **NUMBERING DEFECT (idea 932's again): a DIFFERENT idea 1097 (`is-the-BREAKEVEN-RUNG-c-star-PREDICTABLE-from-BINDING`) sits at the TOP of '## Open' and is untouched by this run.**

  **THE TWO DIALS AND NO MORE (PROTOCOL rule 4):** CLAIM SET {STRICT = a committed EDGE figure that is a book-minus-null CAGR advantage AND machine-readable in a committed CSV with a fully determined cell; WIDE = STRICT plus every committed prose unit carrying an `edge` token, a pp figure AND a null token} x NULL GATE {OPEN = random ranks, elig = all priced — 1071's/1082's/1086's convention, i.e. the one every committed EDGE figure was measured under, reproduced as a CONTROL under each committer's exact seed recipe; ELIG = random ranks, elig = the book's own 200d/vol gate, so the null differs from the book only in ORDERING}. **THE CELL COORDINATES ARE NOT DIALS** — (panel, N, H, cap, seeds, DD-match convention) are taken exactly as the record committed them; this run walks no new rung. Frozen at 936/1071/1082/1085/1086's construction: CAND20 legs, max_vol 0.60, gross 0.75, W cadence, 10 bps, LAG 1.

  **THE HARVEST. 94 committed EDGE figures, 188 prices, all published.** FAMILY A (1082/1085/1086): 2 panels x 9 N x 3 H, cap INF, 40 seeds, REBUILT match = 54. FAMILY B (1071): 2 panels x 4 N x 5 caps, H=126, 20 seeds, CASH match = 40. **The record had scored exactly 18 of them against the ELIG null (1085, H=126, cap INF); 76 had NEVER been re-priced** — the whole H=21 and H=63 slices and the entire cap ladder, where the null gate had never been crossed with the cap dial at all.

  **THE ANSWER.** `GATE = EDGE_OPEN - EDGE_ELIG` is **<= 0 at 81 of 94 committed figures (0.862)**, mean **-0.791 pp**, median -0.679, range **[-2.728, +1.104] pp**. Against the seed SE of the difference, **50 of 94 are decisively negative at 2 SE, 2 decisively positive, and 42 cannot tell.** So most committed EDGE figures are lower bounds, decisively so; a minority are upper bounds; and roughly 45% of the record is inside its own noise. **The 13 exceptions are NOT scattered: 6 of 9 sit in ONE slice, U56 at min hold 21**, where mean GATE is **+0.216 pp** and the ELIG null is the EASIER comparand. Every other (panel, H) slice reads 8/9 or 9/9 negative (B136 -1.902 / -1.815 / -0.869 pp at H = 21 / 63 / 126). **H_LOWER FAILS**, and it fails in one identifiable place rather than everywhere.

  **H_MAGNITUDE FAILS.** 1097's queue text quotes 1085's **0.03-2.47 pp** as the size of the understatement. Three cells fall outside it, all B136: N=15/H=21 **-2.728**, N=12/H=63 -2.487, N=5/H=126 -2.471 pp. The envelope was a property of the 18 cells 1085 happened to measure, not of the record.

  **WHAT ACTUALLY CHANGES — sign, rank, verdict.** **H_SIGN PASSES: 0 of 94** committed figures change sign; no published EDGE reading is reversed. **H_VERDICT fails on 3 of 94**, all the same way (not-decisive -> decisive, all at N=40; decisive count 91 -> 94) — the re-read RESCUES the record's weakest EDGE figures rather than overturning its strongest. **H_RANK FAILS HARDEST AND IS THE FINDING THAT MATTERS: 13 of 34 committed LADDERS change their argmax**, against the single U56 H=126 case (12 -> 5) 1085 had already published. **Twelve of the thirteen sit on axes the record has never scored against this null: the H axis (7 of 18 move, rho down to -0.500) and 1071's cap axis (5 of 8, rho down to -0.800, three ladders at NEGATIVE rank correlation). The N axis — the one the record has walked most — is the stable one (1 of 8, rho +0.867 to +1.000).** Read beside idea 1098, which showed the argmax is not stable to the DRAW, this says it is not stable to the CHOICE OF NULL either: **a published argmax on an H or cap ladder should not be quoted without its null gate.**

  **H_CONV FAILS 7 of 8, AND THE FAILURE IS AT 1071's OWN HEADLINE.** At the doubly-committed U56/N=20/H=126/cap-INF cell — 1071's committed **+5.07 pp**, reproduced here exactly at 5.0719 — GATE reads **+0.271 pp under CASH** and **-0.027 pp under REBUILT**: the two DD-match conventions **disagree on the sign** (max |CASH - REBUILT| 0.386 pp over the 8 cells). At the record's most-cited EDGE figure, "is it a lower bound?" is not a tape question at this resolution. **1071's +5.07 should be quoted as +/-0.4 pp for convention alone**, before any sampling error.

  **THE CLAIM-SET DIAL: WIDE ADDS NO PRICES.** Corpus **31,030 committed units** (LEADERBOARD and QUEUE by line, CHANGELOG and 863 `*.result.md` by paragraph). **1,019 carry an `edge` token, 1,001 of those a numeral, 215 a figure in pp, 161 a null token — and only 52 carry BOTH a pp figure and a null token**, 35 of those a cell key. The record's `edge` vocabulary is overwhelmingly NOT the null contrast ("grid edge", "knife-edge", "ladder edge", "Sharpe edge"), and the 52 that are trace back to the same four runs whose CSVs STRICT already harvests at full precision. Re-pricing a prose figure adds a rounding error, not a measurement.

  **A RECORD-HYGIENE DEFECT THIS RUN HIT AND PAID FOR.** 1071's `cap` column is a STRING KEY ("INF", "1.00", "1.25", "1.50", "2.00") that enters its md5 null-seed recipe VERBATIM. Reading that CSV with pandas' default dtype silently converts it to `inf / 1.0 / 1.25 / 1.5 / 2.0`; re-formatting those draws DIFFERENT nulls. The first pass of this script reproduced 1071 at **8 of 40 cells** (only the `1.25` rung, whose string round-trips) and failed its cross-run gate at 8.98e-03 — **plausible wrong numbers, not an error.** The column is now read as text and never converted. This is open idea 979's `unit`/`cell_key` proposal arriving with a measured cost; filed as 1105.

  **GATES 13 of 13 PASS, printed before any result number.** G1/G1b/G1c fast runner, `gross_rescaler(1.0)` and the CAPPED build all == `engine.backtest` at 1.39e-17; G2 936/1071/1082's committed W/H126 N=20 triple 3.18e-07; G3 SPY OOS triples 1.70e-04 / 4.05e-05; G4 live RULES v2 MaxDD == -12.05% / -12.24% at 4.95e-05 / 9.85e-06. **The point of the suite is G5-G9: 1086's 54 committed EDGE_pp at 8.88e-16, 1085's 18 EDGE_ELIG_pp and EDGE_OPEN_pp at 8.88e-16, 1071's 40 committed null medians and book CAGRs at 8.33e-17, 1082's 18 at 8.88e-16, and every harvested figure against its own committer at 8.44e-15 — so the OPEN column is literally the record's own numbers and every GATE is a difference of two measurements made the same way.** HYPOTHESES 2 of 7 (H_SIGN, H_WF pass; H_LOWER, H_MAGNITUDE, H_RANK, H_VERDICT, H_CONV fail and are the results above).

  **POST-RUN DIAGNOSTIC, LABELLED AS POST-RUN, AND IT CUTS AGAINST THIS RUN'S OWN HEADLINE.** lambda is constrained to (0, 1], so a null draw already drier than the book enters unmatched at lambda = 1 with its CAGR understated, which INFLATES EDGE. **The ELIG null is drier far more often: clip share 0.609 against OPEN's 0.344, and ELIG clips more at 80 of 94 cells.** That inflates EDGE_ELIG more than EDGE_OPEN and pushes GATE NEGATIVE — toward the "everything is a lower bound" conclusion being tested. **H_LOWER fails anyway, at 13 cells, several with the bias at its strongest** (U56 N=15/H=21: clip 0.000 OPEN vs 0.925 ELIG, GATE still +0.640 pp). The bias is conservative for the failure; it is NOT conservative for the 81 negative cells, whose magnitudes are upper bounds on the true understatement.

  **RULE 8 AND BOTH KEEP PATHS.** EDGE is not a KEEP path and this run's dial 2 CANNOT move one: the book at every cell is byte-identical across the two gates, only the comparand changes, so 4a/4b are mathematically invariant to the re-read. Both are scored at all 94 cells anyway. Cell chosen on IS 2009-2016 ALONE by four choosers, OOS 2017-2026 read ONCE, 8 picks: **H_WF PASSES 2 of 8 — and NEITHER is an EDGE chooser.** C_ISSHARPE picks U56 N=12/H=21 (OOS 17.57% / 1.1426 / -19.48%, 4b OOS PASS) and C_ISDD picks U56 N=40/H=21 (13.01% / 1.1286 / -19.27%, PASS); **both EDGE choosers, under EITHER gate, pick the same losing cell N=5/H=63 on BOTH panels and fail on the drawdown leg** — 1082's and 1084's reading reproduced from a third direction: re-pricing the EDGE ladder against the right null does not give it out-of-sample selection information a plain IS Sharpe does not carry better. Benchmarks: U56 SPY full 15.10% / 0.8829 / -33.72% (halves 0.9588/0.8207), OOS 15.21% / 0.8711 / -33.72%; RULES v2 live full 8.62% / 1.2007 / -12.05%, OOS 9.45% / 1.2762 / -12.05%; B136 SPY full 15.16% / 0.8861 / -33.72%, OOS 15.33% / 0.8767 / -33.72%, RULES v2 7.98% / 1.0993 / -12.24%, OOS 7.88% / 1.1059 / -12.24%. **4a 0 of 94** — no cell clears the live book's drawdown, a book fact, not a null fact. **4b full-sample 18 of 94, 4b OUT OF SAMPLE 19 of 94, both 18 of 94. NOTHING IS PROPOSED and no memo is written:** all 18 are cells the record already committed and already parked (8 in family A, including 1086's rule-8-reachable U56 N=12/H=21 reproduced at 8.88e-16 and 1082's three; 10 on 1071's cap ladder), **binding on drawdown by 0.01-1.45 pp against the 20.23% cap**, and idea 1083 measured the 90% width of exactly that quantity at 4.1-7.2 pp on this tape. This run inherited those books; re-pricing their nulls says nothing about whether they are capital.

  **SURVIVORSHIP (rule 9).** U56 and B136 are CURRENT-CONSTITUENT panels, so every level is optimistic and every 4a/4b count an UPPER bound. EDGE_OPEN, EDGE_ELIG and GATE are within-pool contrasts over the same tape and the bias very largely cancels out of them; it does NOT cancel out of the 4b legs, measured against SPY, a real index, so the 18 parked passes are flattered by it.
