- 2026-09-16 (lane C, idea 1102 does-the-RESOLUTION-FLOOR-CLAUSE-change-any-committed-ARGMAX-in-the-record) — **ANSWERED = YES, AND THE CLAUSE IS NOT AN EDGE FACT. 26 of 32 rebuildable committed-ladder ARGMAXES fall below their own ladder's 90% sign-resolution floor and become TIE SETS, and the 200 VALUED argmax claims harvested from the corpus go with them at 0.844. KILL of ARGMAX-AS-A-POINT across the record (not just over EDGE), CONFIRM of 1098's two committed triples at 4.97e-05 / 2.13e-05, an independent reproduction of 1096's D3 2-of-4 reachability, and a CORRECTION to this run's OWN declared mechanism. Clause still PROPOSED and NOT enacted (rule 6). No RULES change, no book promoted, no PROTOCOL edit; RULES.md, PROTOCOL.md, scan.py, bot.py and baseline.py untouched.** SELECTION: lane C takes the SECOND open idea; 1102 was the second line under '## Open' and names no EDGAR / Form 4 / 8-K / options / live-data source.

  **THE TWO DIALS AND NO MORE (PROTOCOL rule 4):** CLAIM SET {CORE, WIDE} x FLOOR CONFIDENCE q {0.80, 0.90, 0.95} = **6 cells, ALL published**. CORE = the 32 (panel, ladder, statistic) argmax objects this tree rebuilds EXACTLY — the four ladder families the record's argmax claims are actually read off (N {5,8,10,12,15,20,25,30,40}, H {21,63,126,252}, GROSS {0.30..0.75}, CADENCE {D,W,M,Q}) on U56 and B136, argmaxed over full Sharpe, OOS Sharpe, full CAGR and MaxDD. WIDE = every VALUED harvested claim; one whose (family, statistic) IS a rebuilt cell is scored on that cell's MEASURED verdict, the rest carry a TRANSFERRED rate **declared in advance as an EXTRAPOLATION and not a re-derivation** (1048's convention), and the three bases are counted separately. Frozen at 1082/1086/1094/1098's construction: CAND20 legs, cap INF, max_vol 0.60, gross 0.75, W, min hold 126, N=20, 10 bps, LAG 1, warm-up 260, IS end 2016-12-31, each except where it is the ladder. **BLOCK LENGTH is NOT a dial:** headline L=63, L in {21, 126} beside it — and all three read **26 of 32**.

  **THE ANSWER.** CORE tie sets **24 / 26 / 27 of 32** at q = 0.80 / 0.90 / 0.95 (0.750 / 0.812 / 0.844). WIDE **150.5 / 168.8 / 173.4 of 200** (0.752 / 0.844 / 0.867), of which **134 are measured-by-family**, 24 transferred-by-statistic and 42 transferred-overall. **H_TIE PASSES and the rival named in advance — that EDGE is simply the record's noisiest laddered statistic and the plain performance statistics resolve — is refuted: full Sharpe, OOS Sharpe, CAGR and MaxDD all fail to resolve their own peaks at the same rate.** Stronger than an unresolved peak: **19 of 32 floors are INFINITE at q=0.90**, i.e. no gap anywhere on the ladder is sign-resolved, so there is no gap size at which that ladder could publish a point at all.

  **D1 — THE ONLY ARGMAXES THIS TAPE RESOLVES ARE GRID EDGES OF A MONOTONE DIAL (post-hoc, labelled, excluded from every count).** All **6 of 6** RESOLVED objects sit on the GROSS ladder and **every one is a ladder END** — CAGR and Sharpe peak at gross 0.75, MaxDD at 0.30 — so grid-edge share is **1.000 among RESOLVED against 0.385 among TIE SETS**. Two of the six resolve on a **whole-ladder Sharpe spread of 0.0040** (B136 GROSS: gap 0.0004 against floor 0.0004): gross is a pure scaling, every rung moves together in every draw, and the resulting resolution says nothing about an interior optimum because there is none. **The record can resolve an argmax exactly where the argmax carries no information.**

  **D2 — WHAT THE CLAUSE ACTUALLY REWRITES.** Not just the verdict: the mean 90% tie set is **4.88 rungs = 0.851 of its own ladder** (median 4, max 10), and **12 of 32 are WHOLE-LADDER** — the clause leaves those argmaxes with no content whatsoever. This is the cost of enacting it, and it is the reason the clause stays PROPOSED here rather than enacted.

  **H_LADDER FAILS 0 of 8, AND THE FAILURE IS THE MECHANISM.** Declared in advance: a longer ladder should carry a HIGHER floor, more pairs meaning more chances of an unresolved one. Wrong in every pair — the 9-rung N ladder resolves relatively BETTER than the 4-rung H ladder in **8 of 8** (panel, statistic) cells. **What a ladder resolves is set by how far apart its BOOKS are, not by how many rungs it has**, which is why the H ladder (four holds of one book) resolves nothing and the N ladder (books from 5 to 40 names) resolves some pairs. **HYPOTHESES 5 of 6:** H_TIE, H_STAT (capped floor/spread DD 0.885 vs Sharpe 1.000, thin), H_PANEL (median cross-panel floor ratio **1.398**, inside the declared 1.5x — the floor is a TAPE fact), H_P (**P(bootstrap argmax == full-sample argmax) < 0.50 in 0.562 of objects, median P 0.460**) and H_HARVEST PASS; H_LADDER FAILS.

  **THE HARVEST, WITH ITS REJECTS.** 3,673 claim words ('argmax', 'peaks at', 'optimum at', 'optimal', 'best') over **1,007 files across 5 sources** -> **200 VALUED** and **3,473 REJECTED** (3,135 carrying no rung value inside the 120-char window — 'best' above all — and 338 naming no statistic), **every reject published with its reason and its context** so the acceptance rule can be re-read. By source LEADERBOARD 89 / RESULTMD 53 / QUEUE 43 / CHANGELOG 9 / MEMO 6; by family N 105 / GROSS 33 / THRESH 23 / CADENCE 18 / H 12 / BAND 9; by statistic S_FULL 95 / EDGE 37 / CAGR 30 / S_OOS 25 / DD 8 / TURNOVER 5. LIMITATION DECLARED: the family and statistic patterns are heuristics over prose, and the dominant reject class is exactly the one this idea cannot price.

  **METHOD.** 1098's machinery minus the null — a statistic with no null attached needs no seeds, so ONE circular-block index is drawn and applied JOINTLY to every rung of a ladder, because a rung-to-rung GAP is a paired quantity over one tape. CAGR and Sharpe are EXACT under block resampling from block sums of log1p, r and r^2; MaxDD is not order-invariant and its bootstrap reconstructs the resampled path. 1,000 draws per (panel, ladder, window). Floor at q = the smallest |gap| strictly above every UNRESOLVED gap that is itself resolved, exactly 1098's definition, with the largest unresolved gap published beside it. Where NOTHING on a ladder resolves the floor is INFINITE and is published as such; for the two RATIO hypotheses it is capped at the ladder's own spread, **declared in advance as a LOWER bound and therefore the direction that understates tie-set-ness** — and no tie-set VERDICT depends on the cap, since a gap never exceeds its ladder's spread.

  **GATES 9 of 9, printed before any result number.** G1 fast runner == `engine.backtest` 1.39e-17; G2 CROSS-RUN 936/1071/1082/1094's committed U56 W/H126/N=20 triple 3.18e-07; **G4 CROSS-RUN 1098's committed U56 n=12 triple 4.97e-05 and G4b its committed B136 n=15 triple 2.13e-05**, so this run's ladders are the same objects 1098 measured; G3 SPY OOS triple 1.70e-04; G5 live RULES v2 MaxDD == committed -12.05% at 4.95e-05; G6 determinism 0.00e+00; G7 bootstrap median CAGR == the sample's at 5.45e-04; G8 the axis is live (Sharpe spread 0.1916 over the 54 cells).

  **RULE 8 AND BOTH KEEP PATHS.** Rung chosen on IS 2009-2016 ALONE per ladder by C_ISSHARPE / C_ISDD / C_ISCAGR, OOS 2017-2026 read ONCE. **4 of 24 picks clear 4b full AND OOS, 0 of 24 clear 4a** — and all four are the SAME cell, the standing anchor U56 / W / H=126 / N=20 / gross 0.75 (15.58% / 1.1397 / -19.13%, OOS 16.97% / 1.1643 / -19.13%), reached from the GROSS and CADENCE ladders and from neither the N nor the H ladder on either panel, **independently reproducing 1096's D3 2-of-4 reachability finding**. Whole grid, 54 cells: **4b full 16, 4b OOS 17, 4a 0**. Benchmarks: U56 SPY full 15.10% / 0.8829 / -33.72% (halves 0.9588/0.8207), OOS 15.21% / 0.8711 / -33.72%; U56 RULES v2 live 8.62% / 1.2007 / -12.05%, OOS 9.45% / 1.2762; B136 SPY full 15.16% / 0.8861 / -33.72%, OOS 15.33% / 0.8767 / -33.72%; B136 RULES v2 OOS 7.88% / 1.1059. **NOTHING IS PROPOSED AS CAPITAL and no memo is written:** every 4b-passing cell is prior art the record already holds (1086/1098's PARKed n=12, the standing anchor, 1083's gross ladder, 1096's quarterly cell), and a resolution floor was never a KEEP path.

  **SURVIVORSHIP (rule 9).** U56 and B136 are CURRENT-CONSTITUENT panels. A gap between two rungs of one ladder contrasts two books over the same inflated tape and the bias very largely cancels out of it and out of the floor; it does NOT cancel out of the 4b legs, measured against SPY, a real index, so the 17 OOS passes above are flattered by it.

  Script `research/backtests/2026-09-16_does-the-RESOLUTION-FLOOR-CLAUSE-change-any-committed-ARGMAX-in-the-record_C.py`, 11 CSVs, console log, 4 LEADERBOARD rows. The measured 90% sign-resolution floor is 3.25 pp of CAGR on U56 and 4.02 pp on B136, while the published EDGE peak leads its runner-up by 0.61 pp and 1.49 pp — 5.3x and 2.7x below the floor of the ladder it is read off. KILL of the EDGE argmax as a publishable point on U56 (P(argmax reproduces) = 0.378, and the bootstrap's own MODE is n=5, not the published n=12), a PASS on B136's point (0.728) with its ladder still unresolved, CONFIRM that 1085/1082's four answers are ONE measurement, CONFIRM of all 18 of 1082's committed EDGE figures at the rounding limit, and a resolution-floor clause PROPOSED and NOT enacted (rule 6). No RULES change, no book promoted, no PROTOCOL edit; RULES.md, PROTOCOL.md, scan.py, bot.py and baseline.py untouched.** SELECTION: this lane takes the LAST open line for idea 2. QUEUE carries TWO lines numbered 1098 (idea 932's defect again) and this is the argmax-resolution one.

  **THE TWO DIALS AND NO MORE (PROTOCOL rule 4):** SEEDS S {5, 10, 20, 40} (nested — the first S of the same 40 draws, so the seed dial moves the PRECISION of the null median and nothing else) x LADDER SPACING {FULL9 = 1082's nine rungs, ALT5 = {5,10,15,25,40}, COARSE3 = {5,15,40}}, every spacing a SUBSET of FULL9 so all three read the same paths — **12 cells per panel, 24 in total, ALL published.** Frozen at 1082/1086's construction: CAND20 legs, cap INF, max_vol 0.60, gross 0.75, W, min hold 126, 10 bps, LAG 1, warm-up 260, IS end 2016-12-31. BLOCK LENGTH is NOT a dial: headline L=63, L in {21, 126} reported beside it and never selected on. EDGE(n) = 100 x (CAGR_book - median_seeds CAGR_null) under 1082's REBUILT convention (each null re-run at the gross lam*0.75 that equalises its realised |MaxDD| with the book's, 34-step bisection). The bootstrap draws circular blocks of trading days and applies ONE index JOINTLY to the book, all 40 nulls and every rung, because EDGE is a paired quantity over one tape; CAGR under block resampling is exact from block sums of log1p. **LIMITATION DECLARED IN ADVANCE: lam is matched once on the real tape and held fixed inside the bootstrap, so every width published here is a LOWER bound** — the conservative direction for a "not resolved" conclusion.

  **THE ARGMAX'S OWN SAMPLING DISTRIBUTION (L=63, 1,000 draws).** U56 FULL9: full-sample argmax 12 / 5 / 12 / 12 at S = 5 / 10 / 20 / 40 with P(argmax == full) **0.546 / 0.517 / 0.286 / 0.378**, and at S=40 the mass reads **5: 0.390, 8: 0.055, 10: 0.095, 12: 0.378, 15: 0.014, 20: 0.068, and exactly 0.000 on 25 / 30 / 40** — the bootstrap MODE is n=5, not the published peak. B136 FULL9: argmax 10 at every seed count with P **0.738 / 0.798 / 0.764 / 0.728** (mass 10: 0.728, 5: 0.146, 15: 0.116, rest <= 0.004). **90% argmax sets {5, 8, 10, 12} (U56, 4 rungs) and {5, 8, 10, 12, 15} (B136, 5 rungs).** Block length barely moves any of it (P = 0.410 / 0.378 / 0.393 and 0.738 / 0.728 / 0.769 at L = 21 / 63 / 126). **H_STABLE FAILS on U56 (0.378 against the declared 0.50) and PASSES on B136 (0.728); H_WIDTH PASSES on both.** The one part of the shape that IS resolved is the far side: n = 25/30/40 carry zero argmax mass on U56, i.e. 1082's "EDGE collapses above ~n=15" survives and its PEAK does not.

  **THE RESOLUTION FLOOR, MEASURED RATHER THAN ASSUMED.** Over all 36 rung pairs, the smallest |dEDGE| above which the bootstrap agrees with the full-sample sign at least 90% of the time: **U56 3.25 pp of CAGR (95%: 4.66), B136 4.02 pp (95%: 4.02)**. Against that, the peak's lead is **+0.61 pp** (U56, 12 over 10) and **+1.49 pp** (B136, 10 over 15) — **H_FLOOR PASSES on both panels.** **15 of 36 and 18 of 36 rung pairs are not sign-resolved at 90%, the largest unresolved gap being 3.00 and 3.86 pp.** What this does NOT kill: EDGE(peak) - EDGE(40) is **6.41 pp (U56) and 6.47 pp (B136)**, far above either floor, so the collapse stays resolved and only the peak's LOCATION dies.

  **THE PROPOSED CLAUSE (proposed, NOT enacted — rule 6 reserves this for Sunday review).** "Any published argmax over a ladder must state (i) the gap between the winning rung and the runner-up in the units of the quantity being maximised, and (ii) the ladder's own resolution floor at 90%, measured by block bootstrap on the same paths. Where the gap is below the floor, the result is reported as a TIE SET — the rungs carrying 90% of the bootstrap argmax mass — and never as a single rung." Applied to the record as it stands it re-expresses 1082's "EDGE peaks at n=12 / n=10" as **U56 tie set {5, 8, 10, 12}** and **B136 tie set {5, 8, 10, 12, 15}**, and leaves 1082's far-side verdict alone.

  **TWO SUPPORTING FACTS, BOTH CUTTING AGAINST BUYING A FIX.** **H_SEED PASSES on both panels: the width is a TAPE fact, not a seed-count fact** — the 90% set is 4 rungs at S=5 and 4 at S=40 on U56 (5 and 5 on B136) while the per-rung EDGE SE falls 2.1x to 3.5x over the same range (U56 n=12: 1.597 -> 0.332 pp). Seeds sharpen the null median and do nothing to the argmax, because what the argmax is uncertain about is the tape. **The SPACING dial changes the ANSWER while RAISING apparent confidence, which is the part that generalises: H_SPACE PASSES on U56** — COARSE3 reports n=**5** at P 0.638 against FULL9's 12 at 0.378, a different argmax with 1.7x the apparent stability — **and FAILS on B136** (0.598 vs 0.728), where the mechanism is blunter still: **the published peak n=10 is not a rung of COARSE3 at all and the coarse ladder confidently reports 15.** Any argmax in the record read off a 3- or 4-rung ladder is suspect for this reason alone.

  **H_1085 PASSES.** 1085/1082 reported the U56 argmax as 12 / 5 / 12 / 12 across seed counts; this run's bootstrap puts **0.546 / 0.517 / 0.286 / 0.378** of the argmax mass on exactly the rung each of them named. "Four ways of asking give three answers" is better read as ONE unresolved answer.

  **RULE 8 AND BOTH KEEP PATHS.** n chosen on IS 2009-2016 ALONE inside each spacing at each seed count by four choosers (C_ISSHARPE, C_ISDD, C_ISCAGR and C_ISEDGE, the last on the IS-window EDGE with IS-matched nulls), OOS read ONCE: **0 of 48 picks clear 4b on U56, 0 of 48 on B136, 4a 0 of 96.** C_ISSHARPE picks n=40 (U56) and 5/8 (B136); C_ISDD 40 and 30/40; **C_ISCAGR and C_ISEDGE pick n=5 on both panels**, reproducing 1082's finding that its own EDGE selector is the worst pick available. Whole ladder: **4b full 2 of 9 on U56 (n=12 17.71% / 1.1692 / -20.17%, halves 1.275/1.088, OOS 18.89% / 1.1758; n=20 15.58% / 1.1397 / -19.13%, OOS 16.97% / 1.1643) and 1 of 9 on B136 (n=15 16.78% / 1.0682 / -19.66%, OOS 17.49% / 1.0458) — 1082's committed 3 of 18, reproduced exactly — and 4a 0 of 18.** Benchmarks: U56 SPY full 15.10% / 0.8829 / -33.72% (halves 0.9588/0.8207), OOS 15.21% / 0.8711 / -33.72%; RULES v2 live full 8.62% / 1.2007 / -12.05%, OOS 9.45% / 1.2762 / -12.05%; B136 SPY OOS 15.33% / 0.8767 / -33.72%, RULES v2 OOS 7.88% / 1.1059 / -12.24%. **NOTHING IS PROPOSED AS CAPITAL and no memo is written:** the two 4b-passing U56 rungs are 1082's already-PARKed cells, no honest IS-only chooser reaches either, and EDGE was never a KEEP path.

- 2026-09-16 (cloud lane, idea 1096 is-RULE-8-REACHABILITY-a-COST-RUNG-object-across-the-record-s-COMMITTED-PICKS) — **ANSWERED = NO, NOT AS A GENERAL FACT. KILL of the queue's own generalisation (only 2 of 8 CORE picks move at or below 25 bps; median MOVE RUNG 71 bps against a median c\* of 117, a ratio of 1.65x and not 5x), CONFIRM of 1094's specific numbers at 0.00e+00, a CORRECTION (1094's "switches away at 15 bps" is the spacing of its own published ladder — on a 1-bp ladder the switch is at 11 bps, same destination), and three structural findings that matter more than the headline. No RULES change, no book promoted, no PROTOCOL edit (rule 6); RULES.md, PROTOCOL.md, scan.py, bot.py and baseline.py untouched.** SELECTION: this lane takes the FIRST open line for idea 1. QUEUE carries TWO lines numbered 1096 (idea 932's defect again) and this is the rule-8-reachability one, not the gate one.

  **THE TWO DIALS AND NO MORE (PROTOCOL rule 4):** CLAIM SET {CORE, WIDE} x COST RUNG {0, 10, 25, 50} bps = 6+2 cells, ALL published. CORE = the four ladder families the record's committed rule-8 runs actually walked — N {5,8,10,12,15,20,25,30,40} (1071/1082/1094), H {21,63,126,252} (1086), GROSS {0.30..0.75, 10 rungs} (1083), CADENCE {D,W,M,Q} (930/931) — on both panels, 8 ladders; WIDE adds the joint (N,H) grid, the book family {TOP5,TOP10,TOP20,EWELIG,BAND03} and a max_vol ladder, 6 more. 146 cells, 48 (panel, ladder, chooser) picks, 192 priced picks, a 201-rung 1-bp cost ladder. **COST IS NOT A DIAL THE BOOK CAN CHOOSE** — every chooser is re-run separately at each rung and only ever sees the cost its own book pays. CHOOSER {C_ISSHARPE, C_ISDD, C_ISCAGR} is REPORTED everywhere and never fitted on; the headline is C_ISSHARPE, the record's default, fixed before the run. FROZEN: CAND20 legs, cap INF, max_vol 0.60, gross 0.75, W, min hold 126, LAG 1, warm-up 260, IS end 2016-12-31, each except where it is the dial. D1-D4 are labelled post-hoc diagnostics, excluded from every count.

  **THE ANSWER.** C_ISSHARPE picks that differ from their own 10-bps pick: **CORE 0/8 at 0 bps, 2/8 at 25, 2/8 at 50, 5/8 within 200 bps; WIDE 1/6 at 25; ALL 3/14 at 25.** C_ISDD 0/14 at 25 and 2/14 at 50; C_ISCAGR 1/14 and 2/14. The only two CORE movers are U56's HOLD ladder at **14 bps** (21 -> 63 -> 126) and U56's CADENCE ladder at **21 bps** (W -> Q); the GROSS ladder's pick does not move anywhere within 200 bps on either panel. **H_MOVE FAILS** (declared: a majority of CORE picks move by 25 bps). **H_5X FAILS, and the two ways of computing it disagree by 3x, which is itself the finding: ratio of medians 117/71 = 1.65x, median of per-ladder ratios 5.04x — but that median is over the 2 of 8 CORE picks where both quantities exist at all.** H_ABSORB PASSES 8 of 8 on CORE (once a CORE pick moves it never returns; 2 of 48 picks flicker, both with a 0-bps move). **H_DIR FAILS 1 of 8** — B136's N ladder moves 8 -> 5 at 82 bps, i.e. toward HIGHER turnover, which is noise and not cost. **H_TURNSPREAD FAILS** — the GROSS ladder spans 1.21-2.90x/yr of turnover and never moves, the CADENCE ladder spans 1.65-4.06x and moves at 21 bps. **H_VERDICT FAILS** — over {0,10,25,50} bps identity moves in 11 of 48 picks while the 4b-full verdict changes in 8 and 4b-OOS in 9.

  **THE CORRECTION, and it is a precision correction rather than a contradiction.** D1 re-runs 1094's EXACT joint grid (H restricted to {21,63,126}) on 1094's OWN published rung ladder {0,5,10,15,20,25,30,40,50,75,100}: the U56 IS-Sharpe chooser first differs from its 10-bps pick at **15 bps — 1094's number, to the bp**. On a **1-bp** ladder the same chooser switches at **11 bps**, to the same destination (5/63). 1094's 15 bps is the resolution of its ladder, not the switch. G4 reproduces 1086/1094's committed candidate triple at **0.00e+00** and G4b its committed breakeven **c\* = 63 bps at 0.00e+00**, so the two runs are measuring the same objects.

  **THREE STRUCTURAL FINDINGS.** **D2 — for 76.2% of picks the question has no referent:** c\*(10-bps pick) is UNDEFINED, the pick already failing 4b at ZERO cost, for **32 of 42** (panel, ladder, chooser) picks, including every one of B136's 21. 1094 generalised from two of the ten that do clear 4b uncharged. **D3 — reachability is a property of the LADDER, not of the book:** the anchor cell (936/1071/1082/1094's W / H=126 / N=20 / gross 0.75 book, the standing top-20 family) is a rung of ALL FOUR CORE ladders, and C_ISSHARPE reaches it from **2 of 4** at 0 and 10 bps on BOTH panels (GROSS and CADENCE), 1 of 4 at 25 bps on U56, 2 of 4 at 50 — while the N ladder picks 40 / 8 and the H ladder 21 / 63 at every rung. Same book, same tape, same cost: reachable or not by which dial was walked, an effect far larger than the cost rung's. **D4 — a stable pick is not a resolved pick:** the IS-Sharpe margin between the 10-bps pick and its runner-up runs 0.0001 to 0.1238, and **8 of 14 picks are decided by less than the record's own committed seed-noise floor (877: 0.0145 of Sharpe)**. The most cost-stable pick in the run, the GROSS ladder's, is decided by **0.0001 / 0.0003** over a whole-ladder Sharpe spread of 0.0014 / 0.0034 — its stability means only that gross barely moves Sharpe, and must not be read as strength.

G2 CROSS-RUN 936/1071/1082/1094's committed U56 W/H126 N=20 triple 3.18e-07. G3 SPY OOS triple 1.70e-04. G4 0.00e+00. G4b 0.00e+00. G5 live RULES v2 MaxDD == committed -12.05% at 4.95e-05. G6 CAGR non-increasing in the rung at all 146 cells (0.00e+00). G7 determinism 0.00e+00. G8 the cost axis is live (5.66 pp of CAGR spread 0 -> 50 bps). G9 the within-ladder turnover spread is live (7.42x/yr).

  **RULE 8 AND BOTH KEEP PATHS.** Every pick chosen on IS 2009-2016 ALONE, OOS 2017-2026 read ONCE, separately at each rung. U56 picks: **4b full 6/8 and 4b OOS 6/8 at 0 and 10 bps, 2/8 at 25, 3/8 at 50; 4a 0/8 at every rung.** B136: **0/8 at 10, 25 and 50 bps** (1/8 at 0 bps on the OOS legs only). Whole grid, 146 cells: 4b full **35 / 33 / 29 / 21** and 4b OOS **39 / 36 / 30 / 27** at 0 / 10 / 25 / 50 bps; **4a 0 of 146 at every rung.** Benchmarks: U56 SPY full 15.10% / 0.8829 / -33.72% (halves 0.9588/0.8207), OOS 15.21% / 0.8711 / -33.72%; RULES v2 live full 8.62% / 1.2007 / -12.05%, OOS 9.45% / 1.2762 / -12.05%; B136 SPY full 15.16% / 0.8861 / -33.72%, OOS 15.33% / 0.8767 / -33.72%; RULES v2 OOS 7.88% / 1.1059 / -12.24%. **NOTHING IS PROPOSED and no memo is written:** every 4b-passing pick at PROTOCOL's 10 bps is an object the record already holds — 1086's PARKed N=12/H=21 candidate (16.80% / 1.1625 / -19.48%, OOS 17.57% / 1.1426 / -19.48%) and the standing top-20 anchor (15.58% / 1.1397 / -19.13%, OOS 16.97% / 1.1643 / -19.13%, c\* = 117 bps, 1094's own figure reproduced). The one new observation is filed as an idea, not a proposal: at 25 and 50 bps the CADENCE chooser moves to QUARTERLY (1.65x/yr turnover) and that cell clears 4b full and OOS at both rungs (15.11% / 1.1203 / -19.94% at 25 bps; 14.64% / 1.0891 / -19.94% at 50) — but no chooser selects it at the 10 bps PROTOCOL actually fixes, so it is not rule-8 reachable there.

  **SURVIVORSHIP (rule 9).** U56 and B136 are CURRENT-CONSTITUENT panels: every level is optimistic, every 4b/4a count an UPPER bound and every c\* an UPPER bound on the cost a real book of this kind could have paid. The PICK half contrasts two selections over the same inflated tape and the bias very largely cancels out of it; it does NOT cancel out of the 4b legs, measured against SPY, a real index.

  Script `research/backtests/2026-09-16_is-RULE-8-REACHABILITY-a-COST-RUNG-object-across-the-record-s-COMMITTED-PICKS_cloud.py`, 8 CSVs, console log, 4 LEADERBOARD rows. - 2026-09-16 (lane C, idea 1094 does-the-U56-n12-H21-CANDIDATE-survive-25-and-50-bps) — **ANSWERED = YES, IT SURVIVES BOTH, AND THE QUEUE'S COST-EXPOSURE PREMISE IS KILLED. The candidate clears every 4b leg full-sample AND out of sample at 25 and at 50 bps, and breaks at 63 bps — on the HALVES SHARPE leg, not the CAGR floor. But the run replaces the objection it removed with a sharper one: the candidate's rule-8 REACHABILITY dies between 10 and 15 bps, roughly FIVE TIMES earlier than the cell itself. Still PARK, still NOT proposed, no RULES change, no book promoted, no PROTOCOL edit (rule 6); RULES.md, PROTOCOL.md, scan.py, bot.py and baseline.py untouched.** SELECTION: lane C takes the SECOND open idea; 1094 was the second line under '## Open' and names no EDGAR / Form 4 / 8-K / options / live-data source.

  **THE TWO TUNED DIALS AND NO MORE (PROTOCOL rule 4).** N {5, 8, 10, 12, 15, 20, 25, 30, 40} x H {21, 63, 126} — 1082/1086's grid verbatim — = 27 cells per panel, 54 in total, ALL published at ALL 11 published rungs (594 (cell, rung) pairs). **COST IS NOT A DIAL THE BOOK CAN CHOOSE.** It is PROTOCOL rule 2's execution assumption; nothing here is selected on it, the rule-8 choosers are re-run SEPARATELY at every rung (each sees only the cost its own book pays), and the verdict is quoted at the harshest rung the queue asked for. Everything else frozen at 936/1064/1071/1082/1086's construction: cap INF, CAND20 legs, max_vol 0.60, gross 0.75, W cadence, LAG 1.

  **THE COST LADDER IS EXACT, NOT RE-RUN.** `engine.backtest` computes `r = (held*rets).sum - turnover * c/1e4` and neither `held` nor `turnover` depends on cost, so one run per cell gives the whole ladder exactly. **G1/G1b/G1c check this against `engine.backtest` at 10, 25 and 50 bps at 1.39e-17** — which is what licenses the 201-rung fine ladder (0..200 bps in 1 bp steps) used to solve each cell's BREAKEVEN rung c\*.

  **THE ANSWER.** U56 n=12, H=21 at 0 / 10 / 25 / 50 / 75 bps: CAGR **17.64% / 16.80% / 15.54% / 13.48% / 11.46%**, Sharpe **1.2131 / 1.1625 / 1.0865 / 0.9595 / 0.8322**, MaxDD **−19.45% / −19.48% / −19.51% / −19.61% / −19.75%**, halves **1.301/1.150 → 1.248/1.102 → 1.167/1.029 → 1.033/0.907 → 0.898/0.785**, OOS **18.45%/1.1919 → 17.57%/1.1426 → 16.26%/1.0685 → 14.11%/0.9448 → 12.00%/0.8209**. Against SPY (full 15.10% / 0.8829 / −33.72%, halves 0.9588/0.8207; OOS 15.21% / 0.8711 / −33.72%; DD cap 20.23%, CAGR floor 10.57%) it **passes all five 4b legs and all three OOS legs at every rung up to and including 63 bps**, and fails at 64 on **L_H1** (H1 Sharpe falling under SPY's 0.9588), with **L_CAGR still clearing by +0.9 pp at 75 bps**. c\*_OOS = 64 bps. The pass set is contiguous from 0 at **54 of 54 cells** (H_MONO PASS), so "breakeven rung" is a well-defined object here. **H_DIE25 and H_DIE50 both FAIL; H_LEG FAILS.** The rival reading declared in advance — that a book binding on drawdown at 10 bps would die on the CAGR floor as cost rose — is wrong: cost reaches the **Sharpe** legs first, because SPY pays no turnover and the book's vol barely moves.

  **THE QUEUE'S 2.4x TURNOVER PREMISE BUYS ONE BASIS POINT.** H_QUEUE technically PASSES — c\*(12,21) = **63** bps against c\*(12,126) = **64** bps — but the gap is **1 bp on a 2.4x turnover ratio** (7.19x/yr vs 3.04x/yr), because the two cells bind on different legs: the H=21 cell on L_H1, the H=126 cell on **L_DD**, which cost barely moves. **D1 quantifies why:** over 0→50 bps the mean cell loses **1.66 to 4.69 pp of CAGR** and gains only **0.07 to 0.51 pp of |MaxDD|** — a ratio of **8x to 42x**. A book whose binding leg is drawdown is nearly cost-immune on that leg; the rung is a statement about the Sharpe and CAGR legs alone.

  **THE MOST COST-ROBUST CELL IN THE FAMILY IS THE INCUMBENT, NOT THE CANDIDATE.** U56 **n=20, H=126** — 936/1071's own construction — clears 4b full and OOS at **every rung to 117 bps** (c\*_OOS 149), at 2.90x/yr turnover: 50 bps leaves it at **14.25% / 1.0535 / −19.35%**. **H_FAMILY FAILS** (c\* spread across the nine cells that pass at 0 bps is **109 bps**, not ≤10), so the rung is emphatically NOT a family-turnover fact: c\* runs 8 / 36 / 38 / 40 / 50 / 63 / 64 / 68 / 117 bps across the passing cells, and **H_CSTAR_TURN PASSES at Spearman(c\*, turnover) = −0.65** — churn predicts cost fragility, but loosely, and the binding leg predicts it better.

  **GATES 12 of 12, printed before any result number.** G1/G1b/G1c the exact cost ladder == `engine.backtest` at 10/25/50 bps, 1.39e-17; G2 CROSS-RUN 936/1071/1082's committed W/H126 N=20 triple 3.18e-07; G3 SPY OOS triple 1.70e-04; **G4 CROSS-RUN 1086's committed candidate — both triples, full and OOS — at 0.00e+00, bit-identical**; G4b/G4c its committed turnovers 7.19x and 3.04x/yr at 3.6e-04 and 6.4e-04; G5 live RULES v2 MaxDD == −12.05% at 4.95e-05; G6 CAGR non-increasing in the rung at all 54 cells, 0.00e+00; G7 determinism 0.00e+00; G8 the cost axis is live (9.11 pp of U56 CAGR spread). **HYPOTHESES 7 of 11** — H_DIE25, H_DIE50, H_LEG and H_WF50 fail, and the first three failures ARE the result.

  **RULE 8, AND THE NEW OBJECTION.** (N, H) chosen on IS 2009-2016 ALONE by three choosers at each of 11 rungs, OOS 2017-2026 read ONCE. **H_WF25 PASSES (1 of 6 picks at 25 bps); H_WF50 FAILS (0 of 6).** The reachable band is **0 to 30 bps**: at 15 bps and above **U56's IS-Sharpe chooser stops picking the candidate** and switches to (n=5, H=63), which fails 4b on drawdown at every rung; from 15 to 30 bps the only surviving reachable pass is C_ISDD's **(n=40, H=21)** (25 bps: OOS 12.18% / 1.0636 / −19.37%), and by 40 bps that chooser moves to (40, 63) and nothing is reachable at all. **So the property 1086 headlined — "the first cell in this family an honest IS-only procedure reaches" — is itself the most cost-fragile thing about the candidate, dying at roughly a fifth of the rung the cell dies at.** **H_HOLD PASSES 6 of 6:** every (panel, chooser) sequence moves weakly to LONGER holds as the rung rises, which is the queue's mechanism showing up where it actually lives — in the CHOICE, not in the cell. **H_4A PASSES: 0 of 594 (cell, rung) pairs clear 4a**, at any cost, on either panel. Grid totals, U56: 4b full **8 / 7 / 7 / 5 / 4 / 1** at 0 / 10 / 25 / 40 / 50 / 75 bps; B136: **1 / 1 / 1 / 1 / 1 / 0**, the lone survivor being (15, 126), c\* = 68 bps. Benchmarks: U56 RULES v2 live **8.62% / 1.2007 / −12.05%** at 10 bps, **8.33% / 1.1631 / −12.09%** at 25, **7.85% / 1.1004 / −12.16%** at 50; B136 SPY full 15.16% / 0.8861 / −33.72%, OOS 15.33% / 0.8767 / −33.72%.

  **D2 — THE CANDIDATE'S COST ROBUSTNESS IS NOT A COIN FLIP.** 40 gross-matched random-rank draws at the same cell (1082/1086's seed recipe, same hold, no eligibility gate) turn over **12.91x/yr** median against the book's 7.19x and clear 4b at **0.050 / 0.025 / 0.000** at 0 / 10 / ≥15 bps; the median null c\* is **9 bps** against the book's **63**, and the book sits at percentile **1.000** of the null c\* distribution. Whatever else is wrong with this candidate, its survival to 63 bps is not something a random book of the same shape does.

  **D3 — THE BENCHMARK ASYMMETRY, BRACKETED RATHER THAN ASSERTED.** PROTOCOL's 4b bar is SPY buy-and-hold, which pays no turnover at any rung, so raising the rung is a one-sided handicap (the record's open idea 1063). Charging SPY the BOOK's own turnover at the same rung — the opposite extreme — flips **no verdict** in the eight (panel, cell, rung) pairs examined: U56 (12,21) and (12,126) pass under both conventions at 25 and 50 bps, B136's fail under both. The convention is one-sided; on this question it is not load-bearing.

  **SURVIVORSHIP (rule 9).** U56 and B136 are CURRENT-CONSTITUENT lists. Every level is optimistic, every 4b count an UPPER bound, and **every breakeven rung c\* published here is an UPPER bound on the cost a real book of this shape could have paid** — the survivorship bias flatters the numerator of the margin and not the turnover in the denominator.

  **WHERE THIS LEAVES THE CANDIDATE.** 1086's memo gave five reasons not to promote. This run **removes reason 5** (cost exposure: the 2.4x turnover ratio costs it 1 bp of breakeven, and it absorbs 50 bps with all eight legs intact) and **adds a better one**: what an IS-only procedure can REACH is far more cost-fragile than what the cell delivers, and above 10 bps the candidate is a cell no honest procedure selects. Reasons 1-4 (undecidable DD margin, panel fragility, unstable hold choice, 0 of 4a) stand untouched. **PARK, memo updated, NOT proposed for capital.**

That is exactly the SLOPE premise 1082 killed, alive at a shorter hold: **1082's CEILING verdict is partly an artefact of its own frozen H = 126.** **On U56 the queue is WRONG.** Argmax reads **12 / 8 / 12** (**H_LEFT FAIL**), the hump survives at every hold, and shortening H makes it SHARPER rather than flatter: **EDGE(12) - EDGE(5) goes from +0.870 +/- 0.507 pp (1.72 SE, NOT decisive, 1082's own number) at H = 126 to +1.696 +/- 0.574 pp (2.96 SE, DECISIVE) at H = 21.** Under staleness a 21-day hold should SHRINK that gap; it grows. **H_E5 passes on both panels but means opposite things** — B136 EDGE(5) runs 5.717 -> 11.269 -> 8.074, U56 5.950 -> 6.637 -> **5.967**, a rise of +0.016 pp against a 0.383 pp seed SE.

  **THE THIRD OUTCOME, NAMED IN ADVANCE, ALSO FAILS.** It was declared before the run that a shorter hold might simply raise EDGE at EVERY n (a pure level effect) without moving the peak. dEDGE = EDGE(n, 21) - EDGE(n, 126) reads **U56 mean +0.184 pp (7 of 9 rungs positive, but the n=5 rung moves +0.016)** and **B136 mean -1.135 pp (1 of 9 positive)**. A shorter hold does not lift the edge uniformly; on B136 it LOWERS it everywhere except n = 5, which is precisely why the peak moves there.

  **A CORRECTION — THE HOLD IS NOT A MONOTONE DIAL.** H = 63 is not between H = 21 and H = 126 on either panel: U56's argmax reads 12 / 8 / 12, and B136's EDGE(5) is HIGHEST at the middle hold (+11.269, against +8.074 at H=21 and +5.717 at H=126). Any reading of the form "as H shortens, X" is unavailable from a three-point ladder on this tape, and the strongest version of the queue's claim — monotone in H — is supported by NEITHER panel. Filed as idea 1095.

KILL of H_STEPLIN and H_STEPREC, a CORRECTION, a CENSUS, and a KILL of this run's OWN declared direction. No book proposed, no RULES change, no PROTOCOL edit (rule 6); RULES.md, PROTOCOL.md, scan.py, bot.py and baseline.py untouched.** SELECTION: the cloud lane takes the FIRST eligible open idea; 1087 was the first line under '## Open' and names no EDGAR / Form 4 / 8-K / options / spin-off / live-data source.

  **THE TWO DIALS AND NO MORE (PROTOCOL rule 4):** CADENCE STEP {D->W, D->M, D->Q, W->M, W->Q, M->Q} x GROSS RUNG {0.25, 0.50, 0.75, 1.00} = 24 dial cells, reported at all 10 (panel, book) cells = **240 published step-points**, off a 160-point turnover ladder. REPORTED and never fitted on: panel {U56, B136}; book {TOP5, TOP10, TOP20, EWELIG, BAND03} — idea 930/1076's set verbatim; cadence {D, W, M, Q}; source {LEADERBOARD, CHANGELOG, QUEUE, RESULTMD}. The record's own ladder is {0.50, 0.75, 1.00}; 0.25 widens the curve and no verdict is read off it alone.

  **THE ANSWER.** `max |(STEP(g)/g)/STEP(1.00) - 1|` reads **1.1831e-01** over the full ladder and **6.4801e-02** over the record's own, against 1076's LEVEL residuals of 5.3116e-02 / 2.8691e-02 reproduced here to 9.08e-06 — an **AMPLIFICATION of 2.227x / 2.259x**. Per rung **@0.25 1.183e-01 / @0.50 6.480e-02 / @0.75 2.464e-02 / @1.00 0**. **H_STEPLIN and H_STEPREC both FAIL** at a 1% bar; **H_AMPLIFY PASSES**, so the queue's clause that the residual does not cancel on a difference is confirmed in direction and priced. The worst cells are the family 1076 already flagged: **BAND03 `D->W` @ g=0.50** (B136 +6.48e-02, U56 +5.68e-02) — the slowest-turning book differenced against the fastest cadence, where drift between decisions has most room.

  **THE BIAS, PRICED.** `BIAS(g->g') = STEP(g)*g'/g - STEP(g')` at the binding 10 bps: over **360 conversions on the record's own ladder, max 4.357 bp/yr, median 0.2346**; over 720 on the full ladder, max 6.646, median 0.3063. By step `D->Q` 4.357 / `D->M` 3.870 / `D->W` 2.669 / `W->Q` 1.689 / `W->M` 1.202 / `M->Q` 0.487 bp/yr. **H_RANK PASSES:** the cadence ordering reads `D > W > M > Q` at all four rungs in **10 of 10** cells — the residual moves sizes, never signs, so no committed "M turns over less than W" claim can be reversed by the rung it is read at.

  **A CORRECTION TO THE QUEUE'S OWN PREMISE.** The queue says every committed cadence comparison differences two books at one gross "so the residual does not cancel". **At the gross it was measured at, a cadence gain is EXACT** — it is the difference of two numbers `engine.backtest` actually produced, and no linearity is assumed, so there is nothing to cancel or fail to cancel. The residual enters only when the gain is **READ AT ANOTHER GROSS**, which is precisely what the 0.924 of committed turnover/drag figures 1076 found unstamped invite. The queue's framing implied a bias where none exists and missed the one that does.

  **THE CENSUS.** Corpus **30,615 committed units**; **312** turnover/drag figures sit in a cadence-bearing unit — **20 STEP** (an explicit `X -> Y`), **143 MULTI** (>= 2 cadences named), **149 ONE** (a level, carried so the denominator is visible). The **163 cadence gains** carry a numeric gross **10 times (0.061)**, and **0 of 20** in the STEP tier do. Against their own stated precision (half a unit in the last printed digit): **MOVED at the median cell 58 of 163 (0.356), at the worst cell 131 of 163 (0.804), not moved even there 32 (0.196)**. **H_PRECISION PASSES (0.644 survive the median bias)** — but it passes because the record prints coarse, not because the tape is kind: every figure printed to one decimal or finer in bp/yr is moved at the median cell. **HONEST LIMIT, stated rather than papered over: 0.939 of these figures state no gross, so no figure can be matched to a cell; the bias is priced over the WHOLE cell population and both the median and the max are published.**

  **GATES 7 of 9, printed before any result number.** G1 fast runner == `engine.backtest` on returns AND turnover 4.16e-16; G2 cost linearity at 5/25 bps 2.08e-17; G3 SPY OOS triple 2.98e-05; G4 live RULES v2 MaxDD == -12.05% at 4.95e-05; G5 CROSS-RUN `turn_yr` vs 1076's committed `linearity.csv` over **160 shared cells at 7.11e-15**; G6 1076's committed 5.312e-02 / 2.87e-02 reproduce at 9.08e-06; G7 determinism 0.00e+00. **G8a AND G8b FAIL AND ARE THIS RUN'S OWN DECLARATION.** It was declared before the run that `REL_step(g)` would be NEGATIVE and that `step/g` would be monotone DECREASING in g. The two halves contradict each other — if `step/g` falls in g then at a low rung it sits ABOVE its own g=1.00 reference, so `rel` is POSITIVE by construction (mean **+8.766e-03**) — and the monotonicity half fails on its own terms: **32 of 60 cells**, where 1076's LEVEL held on **40 of 40**. Differencing two monotone curves of different curvature need not be monotone. Both failures are published beside the right answer rather than replaced.

The next-strongest grid pass, U56 `TOP20 M@0.75` (full 15.28%/1.2125/-19.51%, OOS 17.53%/1.3061/-19.51%), is the record's single most-claimed cell. Both paths are scored because rule 4 requires it on every run. **No memo written.**

The census half is a statement about committed TEXT and is unaffected; the tape half is flattered exactly as every other run on these panels is, and every 4b count above is an UPPER bound. Plus a CORRECTION to this idea's own premise — gross is NOT an exact linear scale on turnover — and a CONFIRM of idea 930's reconciliation, exact on the ladder medians. No book promoted, no RULES change, no PROTOCOL edit (rule 6); RULES.md, PROTOCOL.md, scan.py, bot.py and baseline.py untouched.** SELECTION: lane C takes the SECOND open idea; 1075 went to lane A and 1076 names no EDGAR / Form 4 / 8-K / options / live-data source, so it runs in the sandbox.

  **THE TWO DIALS AND NO MORE (PROTOCOL rule 4):** CLAIM SET {STRICT = a committed unit carrying a turnover/drag token AND a figure in a convertible unit; WIDE = the token and >= 1 numeral} x GROSS CONVENTION {AT100 per unit of gross, AT075 the live book's gross, LADMED the median over the record's own {0.50, 0.75, 1.00}} = 6 cells, ALL published. REPORTED and never fitted on: stamp window {80 chars, 200 chars, whole unit}; source {LEADERBOARD, CHANGELOG, QUEUE, RESULTMD}; panel {U56, B136}; book {TOP5, TOP10, TOP20, EWELIG, BAND03} — idea 930's own set reused verbatim; cadence {D, W, M, Q}; gross ladder {0.25, 0.50, 0.75, 1.00}, where 0.25 exists only to widen the linearity test and no verdict is read off it.

  **THE CENSUS.** Corpus **30,542 committed units** (LEADERBOARD rows, CHANGELOG paragraphs, QUEUE lines, 856 `*.result.md` by paragraph); **2,309 carry a turnover-or-drag token, 751 carry a figure in a convertible unit.** Stamped with a NUMERIC gross: STRICT **0.031 / 0.076 / 0.168** at the 80-char, 200-char and whole-unit windows; WIDE **0.093 / 0.107 / 0.137**. The most generous reading anyone could defend — the bare word `gross` anywhere in the unit — tops out at **0.349**. By source at 200 chars: LEADERBOARD 28/261 (0.107), RESULTMD 23/341 (0.067), QUEUE 6/149 (0.040), and **CHANGELOG ZERO: as committed this file is 6,054 bytes / 9 paragraphs (rotated to the latest entries) and carries no turnover figure at all** — worth knowing before any future run treats it as "the record" (filed as idea 1085).

  **THE RE-EXPRESSION.** 694 unstamped valued figures (148 in `bp/yr`, 546 in `x`) re-expressed under all three conventions x the record's own ladder as the assumed measurement gross, per figure, in `..._C.reexpress.csv`. Two things the dials revealed rather than measured, both results and not bugs: **WIDE adds NOTHING** to the re-expression (1,558 of the 2,309 units carry the token and a numeral but no figure in a convertible unit — you cannot re-express what was never stated in a unit), and **LADMED is EXACTLY AT075** because median{0.50, 0.75, 1.00} == 0.75 == the live book's gross, so the record has **two** gross conventions here, not three.

  **THE CORRECTION.** The queue text says "gross scales turnover linearly". It does not, exactly. `max |(turn(g)/g)/turn(1.00) - 1|` over 40 (panel x book x cadence) cells reads **5.312e-02**, per rung **@0.25 5.31% / @0.50 2.87% / @0.75 1.08% / @1.00 0** (H_LINEAR FAIL); converting with `v(g') = v(g)*g'/g` is wrong by up to **5.31%** over 480 ordered pairs and **2.87%** over the record's own ladder, median 0.69% (H_CONVERT FAIL). The mechanism was declared before the run: `engine.backtest` renormalises the drifted row by `V = 1 + g*d`, so a larger gross deflates it more and pulls it back toward target, making `turn(g)/g` FALL. **G6a (direction) PASSES on 40 of 40 cells; G6b (magnitude, declared under 1%) FAILS at 5.31% and the wrong half is published beside the right one rather than replaced.** The worst cells are all `BAND03` DAILY — the slowest-turning book, where drift between decisions has the most room. **So the stamp removes a factor-of-2.000 ambiguity and leaves a <= 2.87% one**, which is why the clause proposed in the memo asks for the gross a figure was MEASURED at rather than a normalised number.

G5 reproduces 930's committed `dragcurve.csv` turnover over **90 shared cells at 3.55e-15**.

- 2026-09-16 (lane B, idea 1082 does-the-SELECTION-EDGE-COLLAPSE-in-n-price-the-INCUMBENT-n=20-or-just-EXPOSE-it) — **ANSWERED = NEITHER. THE EDGE IS A HUMP AND n=20 SITS ON ITS FAR SIDE. KILL of the queue's own SLOPE premise (H_SLOPE FAIL on both panels), KILL of "n=20 is a chosen optimum" (H_TOP PASS on both), and a PARK stated plainly and NOT proposed. No RULES change, no book promoted, no PROTOCOL edit (rule 6); RULES.md, PROTOCOL.md, scan.py, bot.py and baseline.py untouched.** SELECTION: lane B takes the LAST open idea; 1082 was the last line under '## Open' and is the first in many runs that prices an actual capital book rather than the record's own text.

  **THE TWO DIALS AND NO MORE (PROTOCOL rule 4):** N {5, 8, 10, 12, 15, 20, 25, 30, 40} x SEEDS {5, 10, 20, 40} (nested — the first S of the same 40 draws, so the seed dial moves PRECISION and nothing else) = 36 cells per panel, 72 total, ALL published. Everything else frozen at 936/1064/1071's construction: cap INF, CAND20 legs, max_vol 0.60, gross 0.75, W cadence, min hold 126, 10 bps, LAG 1. N = 20/25/30/40 reproduce 1071's ladder exactly so the two runs read against each other.

  **THE ANSWER.** EDGE(n) = book CAGR minus the median CAGR of that rung's OWN gross- and holding-count-matched null, every null path REBUILT at the gross that equalises its realised |MaxDD| with the book's. It runs **U56 +5.95 / +5.35 / +6.21 / +6.82 / +5.22 / +5.12 / +2.95 / +1.55 / +0.41 pp** and **B136 +5.72 / +5.24 / +8.32 / +6.52 / +6.83 / +4.93 / +4.27 / +2.81 / +1.85** over N = 5..40. **The peak is n=12 (U56) and n=10 (B136) — not 5, and not 20.** Against n=20 the peak is **+1.70 +/- 0.44 pp (3.9 SE)** and **+3.39 +/- 0.42 pp (8.1 SE)**: the record genuinely stopped short, and the queue's suspicion that n=20 was never tested against anything smaller is correct. But the queue's mechanism is wrong. **EDGE does NOT keep rising to n=5**: peak minus EDGE(5) is +0.87 +/- 0.51 pp (1.7 SE, NOT decisive) on U56 and +2.61 +/- 0.58 pp (4.5 SE, decisive) on B136. The pre-declared rival — a CEILING, where the top of the ranking is thin while idiosyncratic variance explodes — is what the tape shows.

  **TWO SUPPORTING FACTS, both of which cut against a simple reading.** (i) **H_DD FAILS:** |MaxDD| is not monotone in n but **U-shaped** (U56 -25.85% at n=5, -19.13% at n=20, -22.98% at n=40), so the book pays for concentration at one end and for common-factor drawdown at the other — 1071's "these drawdowns are common-factor" seen from the other side. (ii) **POST-RUN D2, labelled as post-run:** lambda is constrained to (0, 1], so a null draw already DRIER than the book enters unmatched at lambda = 1 with its CAGR understated, which INFLATES EDGE. That clip binds on **0.57 / 0.60 of draws at n=5 and 0.72 / 0.45 at n=40** and on 0.00-0.38 in between — i.e. it inflates exactly the two rungs the hump verdict needs BELOW the peak. The bias runs AGAINST the conclusion and the hump survives it anyway.

  **GATES 11 of 11, printed before any result number.** G1 fast runner == `engine.backtest` 1.39e-17; G1b `gross_rescaler(1.0)` == `nrun` 1.39e-17 (the bisection kernel is the same book, not an approximation of it); G2 CROSS-RUN 936/1071's committed W/H126 N=20 triple 3.18e-07; G3 SPY OOS triple 1.70e-04; G4c |MaxDD| of the REBUILT null monotone in lambda 0.00e+00; G5a/G5b CROSS-RUN 1071's committed N=20 book CAGR and null median 3.18e-07 / 1.26e-07 — **1071's headline +5.07 pp reproduces exactly**; G6 live RULES v2 MaxDD == -12.05% 4.95e-05; G7 determinism 0.00e+00; G8 the n dial is live (6.73 pp of U56 |MaxDD| spread). **G4 REPAIRS 1071's ACKNOWLEDGED FAILURE:** 1071 declared that scaling a null path by lambda equals rebuilding it at gross lambda*g, then published that the declaration was wrong because the cash sleeve does not drift. This run computes both conventions at every rung and prices the gap at **at most 0.044 pp of CAGR** — the assertion is still wrong, but on this ladder it is immaterial, and both columns are published so no one has to take that on trust.

  **RULE 8 (N chosen on IS 2009-2016 ALONE by four choosers, OOS 2017-2026 read ONCE), AND THE PARK.** **H_WF FAILS 0 of 8 picks.** C_ISSHARPE and C_ISDD pick n=40 on U56 (OOS 14.00% / 1.0983 / -22.98%) and n=8 / n=30 on B136 (14.56% / 0.7866 / -24.54% and 16.61% / 1.0696 / -24.35%). **C_ISEDGE — this idea's OWN selector — picks n=5 on BOTH panels and is the worst pick on B136** (OOS 15.03% / 0.7687 / -28.12%, Sharpe below SPY's 0.8767), and **H_ISEDGE FAILS** (IS-chosen 5 against full-sample argmax 12 / 10). Every pick fails 4b OOS on the drawdown leg. Benchmarks: SPY full 15.10% / 0.8829 / -33.72% (halves 0.9588 / 0.8207), OOS 15.21% / 0.8711 / -33.72%; RULES v2 live full 8.62% / 1.2007 / -12.05%, OOS 9.45% / 1.2762 / -12.05% (B136: SPY 15.16% / 0.8861 / -33.72%, OOS 15.33% / 0.8767 / -33.72%; RULES v2 7.98% / 1.0993 / -12.24%, OOS 7.88% / 1.1059 / -12.24%). **4a 0 of 18 rungs** (H_4A PASS — no n clears the live book's drawdown; that leg is a book fact, not an n fact). **4b full-sample 3 of 18** — U56 n=12 (17.71% / 1.1692 / -20.17%, halves 1.275/1.088), U56 n=20 (15.58% / 1.1397 / -19.13%) and B136 n=15 (16.78% / 1.0682 / -19.66%) — all three also clearing their three OOS legs. **All three bind on DRAWDOWN, by 0.06 / 1.10 / 0.57 pp against the 20.23% cap**, and 1083 measured the 90% width of exactly this quantity at 4.1-7.2 pp on this tape. Not decidable, and not reachable by any honest IS-only procedure: **PARK, NOT KEEP, NOT proposed, no memo written.** The edge below n=20 is real and decisive against its own null; it is not capital.

  **SEED DIAL.** Rank-correlation of the EDGE ladder against S=40 runs 0.90-1.00 at every S, and the B136 argmax reads n=10 at all four seed counts. **H_SEED FAILS on one cell only** (U56 argmax 12, 5, 12, 12 at S = 5, 10, 20, 40): the peak's LOCATION is stable to +/- one rung, its exact argmax flickers below 20 seeds, and no one should quote an argmax from 5 or 10 seeds.

  **SURVIVORSHIP (rule 9).** U56 and B136 are CURRENT-CONSTITUENT panels, so every level is optimistic and every 4b count an UPPER bound.   **THE TWO DIALS AND NO MORE (PROTOCOL rule 4):** BLOCK LENGTH L {21, 63, 126, 252} x DRAWS D {200, 1000} = 16 cells, ALL published, on a fully published 10-rung gross ladder (0.30..0.75) at 936/1064/1071's W/H126 top-20 construction. One circular block index per replicate is applied JOINTLY to SPY, the LIVE RULES v2 book and all ten rungs, because every term of MISS = max(|MaxDD_book| - |MaxDD_live|, 0.70*CAGR_SPY - CAGR_book) is measured on the same tape and a book-only resample would hold both bars fixed and understate the interval.

  **DECIDABILITY RULE, FIXED BEFORE THE RUN:** the claim is decidable at 90% iff the 5th percentile of MISS over the draws is > 0; a straddle means UNRESOLVED.

  **THE ANSWER.** On **MISS\***, the min-over-rungs statistic 1064 and 1071 actually published, the empty-window verdict is **DECIDABLE IN 0 OF 16 CELLS**. U56 90% intervals run **[-0.97, 3.80] -> [-0.75, 3.64] -> [-0.26, 3.41] -> [-0.25, 3.51]** over L = 21 / 63 / 126 / 252 at D=1000; B136 **[-0.89, 4.44] -> [-0.32, 3.21]**; **P(some rung opens the window) 0.076 to 0.165** in every cell. At the single rung g\* the verdict is decidable in only **9 of 16** cells and it **MOVES WITH THE BLOCK LENGTH** (U56/D=1000 reads F,F,T,T; B136/D=200 reads F,T,T,F — **H_STABLE FAIL**) and **WITH THE DRAW COUNT** (4 of 8 (panel, L) cells flip between 200 and 1000 — **H_DRAWS FAIL**). **H_WIDTH PASSES and is the cleanest statement of the result: the 90% width of MISS at g\* runs 4.1 to 7.2 pp against a realised MISS of 1.00 pp** — the record has been adjudicating a one-point quantity with a five-point ruler.

  **A CORRECTION TO THE QUEUE TEXT'S OWN PREMISE.** 1083's own wording says both optima bind on the 4b CAGR floor rather than the DD cap. On a 10-rung ladder that holds only for B136 (g\*=0.45, CAGR leg 1.01 pp vs DD leg 0.58). **The U56 optimum MOVES from g=0.45 to g=0.50** (MISS 1.270 -> **1.002 pp**) once the ladder is refined from 1071's five rungs to ten, and there **the DD leg binds** (1.01 pp against the CAGR leg's 0.23 pp). Across draws **DD binds on 0.718** pooled (0.841 U56, 0.583 B136) — **H_CAGRLEG FAIL.** 1071's "the CAGR floor binds" was a property of its RUNG SPACING, not of the book, and is corrected here by the run that inherited it.

  **GATES 6 of 7, printed before any result number.** G1 CROSS-RUN 936's committed W/H126 triple 3.18e-07; G2 fast runner == `engine.backtest` 1.39e-17; G3 SPY OOS triple 1.70e-04; G5 1071's committed optimum MISS, B136 2.76e-05 and U56 2.68e-01 (the U56 gap is the ladder refinement above, not an error, and is reported as such); G6 live RULES v2 MaxDD == committed -12.05% at 4.95e-05. **G4 FAILS AND THE DECLARED DIRECTION WAS BACKWARDS:** it was declared in advance that block resampling would BREAK long declines and bias bootstrapped |MaxDD| SMALL; it does the opposite, CONCATENATING bad blocks, and the bias reads **+1.54 / +0.87 / +0.05 / +0.00 pp at L = 21 / 63 / 126 / 252** (vanishing monotonically as the worst episode comes to fit inside one block). **The wrong declaration is published beside the failure rather than replaced**, and the direction is stated because it matters: the bias INFLATES MISS and therefore pushes the verdict TOWARD "empty" — the window still cannot be shown empty, so the failure is conservative for the conclusion.

  **RULE 8 (gross chosen on IS 2009-2016 MISS ALONE, OOS 2017-2026 read ONCE), AND THE PARK.** U56 picks g=0.50 -> OOS **11.25% / 1.1633 / -13.06%**; B136 picks g=0.45 -> OOS 9.62% / 1.0071 / -12.82%. Benchmarks: SPY full 15.10% / 0.8829 / -33.72% (halves 0.9588 / 0.8207), OOS 15.21% / 0.8711 / -33.72%; RULES v2 live full 8.62% / 1.2007 / -12.05%, OOS 9.45% / 1.2762 / -12.05% (B136: SPY OOS 15.33% / 0.8767 / -33.72%, RULES v2 OOS 7.88% / 1.1059 / -12.24%). **H_WF PASSES 1 of 2: the U56 pick clears 4b OUT OF SAMPLE outright**, and full-sample it clears **four of 4b's five legs** (H1 1.2030 and H2 1.0960 against SPY's 0.9588 / 0.8207; MaxDD -13.06% against the -20.23% cap), **failing ONLY the CAGR floor, by 0.23 pp** (10.34% against 10.57%); it fails 4a on drawdown by 1.01 pp. It is filed **PARK, NOT KEEP, and is not proposed**: 4b's bar is full-sample and it does not clear it, and this run's own headline is that a margin that size is not decidable on this tape — which cuts against promoting it exactly as hard as it cuts against declaring it dead. **4a 0 of 20 rungs. No book promoted, no memo written.**

  **SURVIVORSHIP (rule 9).** U56 and B136 are CURRENT-CONSTITUENT panels, so every level is optimistic, every MISS published here is a LOWER bound on the true one and every 4b count an UPPER bound. The bootstrap resamples the same inflated tape and prices SAMPLING error only — it cannot and does not correct that bias, and the PARK above is flattered by it.

  Script `research/backtests/2026-09-16_is-the-RESIDUAL-MISS-at-the-GROSS-OPTIMUM-DECIDABLE-at-this-SAMPLE-LENGTH_cloud.py`, 6 CSVs, 4 LEADERBOARD rows. No follow-ups filed beyond 1081/1082 (already open from this run's idea 1): the natural next question — walk n below 20 — is 1082.


- 2026-09-16 (cloud lane, idea 1108 is-BOOK-DISTANCE-rather-than-RUNG-COUNT-what-a-LADDER-s-FLOOR-measures) — **ANSWERED = NO ON THE PREMISE, YES ON THE QUESTION. KILL of 1108's own premise (book distance is not what the floor measures, and the sign runs backwards); CORRECTION to 1102's H_LADDER reading; a REPLACEMENT for the bootstrap on two of four statistics, pre-registered on three legs and passing all three; and a DEFECT in 1102's seeding. No RULES change, no book promoted, no PROTOCOL edit (rule 6); RULES.md, PROTOCOL.md, scan.py, bot.py and baseline.py untouched.** SELECTION: this lane takes the FIRST eligible open idea.

  **THE TWO DIALS AND NO MORE (PROTOCOL rule 4):** DISTANCE STATISTIC {RETCORR, OVERLAP, TURNGAP} x LADDER {N, H, GROSS, CADENCE} = 12 cells, ALL published, on both panels. PANEL is not a dial. BLOCK LENGTH is not a dial: headline L=63, L in {21, 126} reported beside it and never selected on (tie sets 26 / 26 / 26 of 32 at all three). Everything else frozen at 1082/1086/1094/1098/1102's construction.

  **THE KILL.** 1102 read its own H_LADDER failure as "the 9-rung N ladder resolves better than the 4-rung H ladder because its rungs are further-apart BOOKS". Measured directly, that is backwards: **Spearman(rel_floor, adjacent-pair book distance) is POSITIVE in 12 of 12 (distance statistic, statistic) combinations, median +0.7214** — the CLOSER a ladder's books, the BETTER it resolves. **H_DIST FAILS 0 of 12; H_BEATS_COUNT FAILS 1 of 12** (rung count is the stronger predictor, median |rho| 0.8460 against distance's 0.7214). The mechanism is visible in one cell: **the GROSS ladder's ten rungs are the SAME BOOK scaled against a cash sleeve — RETCORR distance 0.0000, OVERLAP distance 0.0000, the smallest any ladder can carry — and it is the ONLY ladder that resolves anything** (median rel_floor 0.109 against 0.961 / 1.000 / 1.000 for N / H / CADENCE; 2 of 8 tie sets against 8 of 8 for each of the other three). It resolves a 0.0002 Sharpe gap while the N ladder cannot resolve a 0.0071 one. **H_RHO PASSES and names the confusion: Spearman(SE_analytic, sqrt(2(1-rho_ij))) = +0.8710 over 558 rung pairs** — distance is (most of) what the SE is made of, exactly as 1108 supposed, but the floor is a GAP-AGAINST-SE quantity and distance enters BOTH terms. 1108 forgot the numerator.

  **THE REPLACEMENT, PRE-REGISTERED BEFORE ANY NUMBER.** An analytic A_ij = Phi(|gap|/SE) built ONLY from the two rungs' own series replaces the bootstrap for a statistic iff it reproduces (i) >= 0.95 of pair RESOLVED verdicts, (ii) >= 0.90 of cell TIE-SET verdicts, and (iii) a floor within 1.5x in median — **all three legs.** The SE is EXACT IN SIGN for CAGR (CAGR is a monotone map of the resampled log-sum) and a delta-method influence function for Sharpe. **H_REPLACE_CAGR PASSES (0.9839 / 1.0000 / 1.0000)** and **H_REPLACE_SHARPE PASSES (0.9624 / 1.0000 / 1.0000)**: **24 of 24 cell tie-set verdicts reproduced with no bootstrap at all, at a median floor ratio of 1.0000.** **S_OOS does NOT replace** — its pair leg reads 0.8871, below the bar; the SEs themselves still agree (Spearman(SE_boot, SE_analytic) 0.9982 / 0.9995 / 0.9993 on CAGR / S_FULL / S_OOS), so what fails is the NORMAL approximation over the shorter OOS window, not the SE. **H_NO_DD, declared and not measured:** MaxDD is a path functional, the resampled ORDER decides it, and no pairwise SE from marginal moments can carry it — **MaxDD keeps the bootstrap, and is where the bootstrap still earns its cost** (6 of 8 DD cells are tie sets under it).

  **THE DEFECT, and it is a record-wide one.** 1102 seeds each ladder with `SEED_BOOT + hash((panel, ladder, L)) % 10_000`. Python randomises `str` hashing per process — `hash(("U56","N",63)) % 10000` returned **8249 / 1895 / 1659** on three consecutive interpreters here — so **1102's floor.csv cannot be regenerated by running 1102.** Re-drawing this run's own floors under 4 seeds moves rel_floor by up to **0.7007 of the ladder spread** (U56 GROSS S_FULL: 0.780 / 0.299 / 1.000 / 0.913); 2 of 32 cells move by more than 0.10, median range 0.0000. **D2, cross-run against 1102's committed floors (L=63, q=0.90): this run's bootstrap reproduces 30 of 32 exactly, its analytic floor 22 of 24, and TIE-SET VERDICTS AGREE 32 of 32.** The honest reading, and the one filed: **1102's CONCLUSIONS survive its own irreproducible seed; its published floor LEVELS should not be quoted to four decimals.** 1102 is corrected, not retracted. This run uses `zlib.crc32` seeds and is reproducible.

  **D1, POST-HOC AND LABELLED.** n = 8 ladders is small and GROSS is an extreme point on BOTH axes at once (lowest distance AND most rungs), so distance and rung count are anti-aligned by construction and this design cannot separate them. Dropping GROSS leaves 6 ladders and two distinct rung counts (9 and 4); on S_FULL every remaining rel_floor is exactly 1.000, so both rank correlations are undefined. **Neither predictor is identified on what remains** — the KILL rests on the sign and on the GROSS mechanism, not on a fitted slope, and is stated that way.

  **GATES 9 of 9, printed before any result number.** G1 fast runner == `engine.backtest` 1.39e-17; G2 CROSS-RUN 936/1071/1082/1094/1102's committed U56 W/H126/N=20 triple 3.18e-07; G3 SPY OOS triple 1.70e-04; G4 1098/1102's committed U56 n=12 triple 4.97e-05 and G4b its B136 n=15 triple 2.13e-05; G5 live RULES v2 MaxDD == -12.05% 4.95e-05; G6 determinism 0.00e+00; **G7 the analytic pair SE == the bootstrap pair SE to 3.26e-02 relative (0.01188 vs 0.01149) — the gate the whole replacement claim rests on, printed before the claim**; G8 the ladders are live (Sharpe spread 0.1916).

  **RULE 8 (rung chosen on IS 2009-2016 ALONE, per ladder, three choosers, OOS read ONCE) AND BOTH KEEP PATHS.** **4 of 24 picks clear 4b full AND OOS; 0 of 24 clear 4a.** All four are the standing anchor (U56 / W / H=126 / N=20 / gross 0.75, full 15.58% / 1.1397 / -19.13%, halves 1.2037/1.0971, OOS 16.97% / 1.1643 / -19.13%), reached from the GROSS and CADENCE ladders only — independently reproducing 1096's D3 and 1102's own 2-of-4 reachability. Whole grid, 54 cells: **4b full 16, 4b OOS 17, 4a 0.** Benchmarks: U56 SPY full 15.10% / 0.8829 / -33.72% (halves 0.9588/0.8207), OOS 15.21% / 0.8711 / -33.72%; U56 RULES v2 live 8.62% / 1.2007 / -12.05%, OOS 9.45% / 1.2762 / -12.05%; B136 SPY full 15.16% / 0.8861 / -33.72%, OOS 15.33% / 0.8767 / -33.72%; B136 RULES v2 7.98% / 1.0993 / -12.24%, OOS 7.88% / 1.1059 / -12.24%. **Nothing new, nothing promoted, no memo written.**

  **SURVIVORSHIP (rule 9).** U56 and B136 are CURRENT-CONSTITUENT panels, so every level is optimistic. A rung-to-rung DISTANCE and a rung-to-rung GAP both contrast two books over the same inflated tape and the bias very largely cancels out of them and out of the floor; it does NOT cancel out of the 4b legs, which are measured against SPY, a real index, so the 16 full-sample 4b passes are an UPPER bound.

- 2026-09-16 (cloud lane, idea 1107 RE-PRICE-every-committed-ARGMAX-on-an-H-or-CAP-LADDER) — **ANSWERED = NONE. NOT ONE of the record's 26 committed H-axis or cap-axis EDGE ARGMAXES survives both tests. KILL of the published H/cap argmax as a claim, CORRECTION to the record's own decisiveness bar, and CONFIRM of 1097 in full. WIDE = the 33 VALUED H-or-cap argmax claims harvested from 1,009 corpus files. THE CELL COORDINATES ARE NOT DIALS — (panel, N, H, cap, seeds, DD-match convention) are taken exactly as committed. BLOCK LENGTH is not a dial: headline L=63, L in {21,126} reported beside it and reading 0 of 26 at ALL THREE.

  **THE ANSWER.** On the headline **TAPE** basis (1098/1102/1108's block-bootstrap sign-resolution floor at q=0.90): **0 of 26 CORE ladders and 0.0 of 33 harvested claims survive both the null gate and their own floor.** On the **JOINT** basis (seed and tape noise in quadrature): **0 of 26.** On the record's **OWN** bar, the seed SE of the null median that 1082 / 1085 / 1097 argued their headlines against: **9 of 26 (0.346).** **The maximum gap-to-floor ratio over all 26 ladders is exactly 1.000** — no committed argmax on either axis clears its own floor even once. Median TAPE floor **1.1493 pp** against a median committed gap of **0.6758 pp**, median gap/floor **0.614**. **18 of 26 ladders resolve NOTHING** (their floor is the whole ladder spread), and only **46 of 134** rung pairs across all 26 ladders are sign-resolved at q=0.90, median pair agreement **0.833** — below the bar.

  **THE FLOOR IS THE BINDING TEST, NOT THE NULL GATE. H_FLOOR_BINDS PASSES: floor-only failures 14, gate-only failures 0**, both fail 12, survive 0. The rank-matched null never kills an argmax the floor would have spared, so 1097's 13-of-34 gate finding — real, and reproduced here exactly — was the smaller of the two problems. **H_SHORT PASSES:** 0 of 18 three-rung H ladders clear their floor, and the 5-rung cap ladders read 0 of 8 as well, so this is not only a rung-count artefact.

  **H_BASIS FAILS AND THAT IS THE CORRECTION.** The three bases agree on the survival verdict for only **0.654** of the 26 ladders, so "survives its floor" is a BASIS statement, not a tape statement — and the disagreement is structural, not noise. The seed SE prices how far the NULL MEDIAN would move if the rank draws were redrawn and prices **nothing at all** about how far the book and the null TOGETHER would move if the TAPE were redrawn. Every committed decisiveness call on these two axes was made with the second term missing, and the 9 SEED-basis survivors (U56 H at N = 8, 20, 25, 40; B136 H at N = 5, 8, 15, 25, 30) are exactly the claims that term kills.

  **WHAT EVERY FORM IS SCORED ON, pre-registered before any number.** A published argmax claim on a k-rung ladder is an ASSERTION SET: it names a set S and EXCLUDES the other k - |S| rungs. **DECIDED** = k - |S| (CONTENT = DECIDED/(k-1)); **EXP_FALSE** = sum over excluded j of (1 - A_peak,j) from the block bootstrap, the expected number of WRONG exclusions published. ERR = EXP_FALSE/DECIDED; **PRICE = DECIDED/EXP_FALSE** is "decidable claims per unit of honesty" read literally. DECISION RULE fixed in advance: among the forms CALIBRATED at q (ERR <= 1-q), the most DECIDED wins.

  **THE ANSWER (q=0.90, 32 CORE cells, 184 decidable rungs).** DECIDED / EXP_FALSE / ERR / PRICE: **AS_WRITTEN 66 (0.359) / 1.41 / 0.0213 / 46.9**, 18 cells contentless, CALIBRATED. WIDER_RUNGS 39 (0.212) / 0.96 / 0.0245 / 40.8, 19 contentless. **ANNOTATED 184 (1.000) / 34.34 / 0.1866 / 5.4 — NOT calibrated.** LONGER_TAPE (M=2, PROJECTED) 90 (0.489) / 1.60 / 0.0178 / 56.1. At q = 0.80 / 0.95 the clause retains 0.516 / 0.293 and leaves 8 / 23 cells contentless. **The literal rule answers LONGER_TAPE at q = 0.90 and 0.95, and that answer is unattainable:** M=2 is 34 years of tape on panels that begin in 2008, and the tape multiple a cell needs for its OWN committed gap to clear its OWN floor has median **12.5x = 220 years**, 8 of 32 not clearing at M <= 500. **Among ENACTABLE forms AS_WRITTEN wins on content AND on price.**

  **BOTH QUEUE ALTERNATIVES, KILLED ON THEIR OWN TERMS. H_ANNOT_PRICE FAILS and its failure is the result:** the point argmax buys 2.8x the content at **24x the wrong exclusions** (34.34 against 1.41) — PRICE 5.4 against 46.9 — so it is dominated, not a trade, and per cell it is calibrated at **NO** confidence (24 / 26 / 26 of 32 cells over their own bar at q = 0.80 / 0.90 / 0.95). **H_COARSE_LOSES PASSES:** keeping every 2nd rung decides 39 against 66, because coarsening leaves a mean 2.50 rungs per cell UNADJUDICATED — a claim cannot rule out what it no longer measures — and ENDS-only decides 10. **H_TAPE_HALF PASSES on a MEASURED exponent, not an assumed one:** floors re-drawn on disjoint sub-tapes at f = 0.25 and 0.50 give a median fitted beta of **-0.4540** over the 24 non-DD cells against 1/sqrt(T)'s -0.50; DD is exempt by declaration and runs the other way (+0.2093), a path functional whose own level grows with the tape.

That is not the set the clause publishes and it never consults q — **it moves between q = 0.80 and 0.95 in 0 of 32 cells while 1102's own capped floor moves in 15.** The queue's premise "4.88 rungs / 0.851 of its ladder / 12 WHOLE-LADDER" reproduces EXACTLY as that mass-set figure over the 26 TIE cells only (4.885 / 0.851 / 12). The clause's own FLOOR set over all 32 cells at q=0.90 is mean **4.69 of 6.75 rungs (0.787)** with **18 of 32 CONTENTLESS**, and it does move with q (17 of 32 cells; 3.78 / 4.69 / 5.06 rungs at 0.80 / 0.90 / 0.95). **Enacted as written the clause costs MORE than 1102 published.** 1102 is corrected, not retracted: its VERDICTS reproduce here 32 of 32.

  **D2 — THE CORRECTION TO THIS RUN'S OWN DECLARED RULE.** The rule declared in (f) tests the AGGREGATE error share, and that test is WEAKER than a per-cell one: a long ladder's far rungs resolve almost surely and dilute the near ones. ANNOTATED is aggregate-calibrated at q=0.80 (0.1866 <= 0.20) and per-cell calibrated at no q. **Under the per-cell reading the q=0.80 answer changes from ANNOTATED to AS_WRITTEN; q=0.90 and 0.95 do not change.** Both readings are published; the per-cell one is the honest bar and is named as such.

  **THE DECLARED APPROXIMATION, AND ITS DIRECTION.** LONGER_TAPE is a PROJECTION: A' = Phi(sqrt(M) Phi^-1(A)) assumes a fixed population gap and an SE falling as 1/sqrt(T). **Both assumptions run TOWARD resolution** — a longer tape would also move the gaps, and a regime it has not seen can only widen the null — so every LONGER_TAPE count here is an **UPPER bound** on what lengthening the tape would buy, and it still loses once its infeasibility is priced. 118 of 224 sub-tape window-cells (18 of 32 on the full tape) carry a floor CAPPED at the ladder spread, where beta describes the SPREAD's scaling rather than the floor's; declared, not hidden.

PROSE arm **9 committed clause / bar / verdict sentences** — a sentence qualifies only if it carries BOTH a whole-ladder trigger phrase AND a decision word, cells read from its committed LINE. **26,323 CSV rows rejected for carrying no INF_FLOOR field, 693 for carrying no flag on the row, and 263 prose sentences rejected (170 NO_LADDER_NAMED, 71 QUOTES_A_FLOOR_NO_DECISION, 22 SMALL panel).   **WHAT REACHABILITY MEANS, STATED BEFORE THE NUMBERS.** An anchor (N, H, gross, cadence) sits on four one-dimensional CORE ladders, each varying one coordinate with the other three frozen at the anchor's own values. A ladder REACHES the anchor under chooser C iff C, given 2009-2016 ALONE, picks the anchor's own rung. The reach COUNT is an integer in 0..4 and 1096's D3 result is "2" for anchor A under `C_ISSHARPE` on U56 and on B136.

  **THE CENSUS OF REACH COUNTS (headline `C_ISSHARPE`).** U56/A **2**, B136/A **2**, SMALL/A **1**, U56/B **0**, B136/B **1**, SMALL/B **0**. The count is 2 in **2 of the 6** cells and the reaching SET `{GROSS, CADENCE}` matches in only those same 2 — H_SAMESET REFUTED. Mean reach count **1.0000** against the uniform-argmax expectation **0.7111** (1/9 + 1/4 + 1/10 + 1/4): above chance by **1.4x**, which is not a level.

  **THE MECHANISM, WHICH IS THE PART WORTH KEEPING.** The GROSS ladder is a boundary attractor under every chooser: IS-Sharpe and IS-CAGR both pick **0.75, the top rung, in 6 of 6** cells; IS-DD picks **0.30, the bottom rung, in 6 of 6**. Anchor A sits at 0.75 and is reached from GROSS on all three panels; anchor B sits at 0.55 and on none. Across all 72 decisions **reach == endpoint-ness on 62 of 72 (86.1%)** — endpoint cells reach **6 of 9**, interior cells **7 of 63**. H_BOUNDARY is REFUTED as a *perfect* predictor (10 discordant cells, all named in the console) and is by far the strongest available one.

H_RESOLVED REFUTED.

CS_STRICT = committed CSV rows whose file indexes the row BY TAPE EXTENT (a sub-tape / fraction / window / tape-length / segment / split key column); CS_HALVES = rows carrying a split-half pair (an H1 and an H2 column, or an IS_ and an OOS_ pair of one statistic) — a half IS a sub-tape, and it is the record's commonest such comparison; CS_ALL is the union. PANEL, LADDER and STATISTIC are NOT dials (3 x 4 x 6 reported everywhere) and the sub-tape fraction is FROZEN at 1140's own {1/1, 1/2, 1/3}.

  **THE CENSUS. CS_STRICT 569 files / 593,172 rows; CS_HALVES 1,788 / 2,140,569; CS_ALL 2,274 / 2,587,354. Publishing the ratio: 1 file, 6 rows.** The header scan covers all 5,529 committed `research/backtests/*.csv` (1.29 GB) and counts rows without parsing values, so the denominator is the whole corpus and not a sample.

  **THE PREMISE HOLDS ON A SECOND CONSTRUCTION, AND UNDER EVERY DEFINITION.** 1140 measured the ratio on its own resolution ratio `|gap|/SD`; this run measures it on the **statistic VALUE** — 27 CORE-ladder books per panel at the standing anchor, 3 panels, 6 statistics, 6 sub-tapes, 486 readings all dumped. Pooled regime/length at R_SPREAD: **CAGR 8.73x, VOL 8.00x, SHARPE 4.39x, MAXDD 2.00x, ULCER 17.51x, CALMAR 8.40x — 6 of 6 above 1**, and 6 of 6 above 1 under R_SD (min 2.69x) and R_MATCHED (min 1.74x). The regime noise is not a 1140 artefact and is not a definition artefact.

  **A CORRECTION TO 1140's OWN BAND, AND THE REASON THE CLAUSE SHOULD NOT COPY ITS DEFINITION.** `max - min` over k points GROWS with k, and 1140's within spread pools a k=2 range (halves) with a k=3 range (thirds). The count-matched form — the mean range of a RANDOM PAIR at every fraction — is smaller in **5 of 6** statistics, median **1.137x**, so 1140's committed **1.7x-to-61x** band is inflated by roughly **14%**. It survives the correction in every SIGN and every VERDICT; the clause should nevertheless require the count-matched form, because the inflation grows with how many stretches a run happens to cut.

  **THE ONLY SUB-1 CELL IN 72 is SMALL / MAXDD under R_MATCHED (0.79x)** — the one place measured here where the length effect exceeds the regime noise, and it sits on the panel whose levels are least trustworthy. Named, not swept, and filed as 1157.

  **PRICING THE CLAUSE — IT COSTS A COLUMN, NOT A RE-RUN. 485 of 569 CS_STRICT files are CHECKABLE (0.8524), carrying 573,668 of 593,172 rows (0.9671)**: their own tape-extent column already takes two distinct values AND repeats one, so both the within spread and the between move can be computed from what is committed. Only **3.29%** of CS_STRICT rows would need a re-run.

  **AND THE WORDING IS THE WHOLE COST — MY OWN SCOPE HYPOTHESIS WAS WRONG. H_SCOPE REFUTED:** CS_HALVES is **3.6x** CS_STRICT in rows, not the >100x declared in advance. The cost is not in the multiple, it is in the SATISFIABILITY: a split-half row can price REGIME (two stretches) but **never LENGTH** (a half is one length), so **0 of 2,140,569 CS_HALVES rows are checkable from what they publish**, and a clause worded over "sub-tape comparisons" would demand a third tape from every one of them — **77.83% of CS_ALL rows needing a re-run against 3.29% of CS_STRICT rows.**

  **THE CLAUSE, DRAFTED (proposed for the Sunday review, NOT enacted — rule 6).** *A published claim whose value depends on the LENGTH or the STRETCH of tape it was computed on — a sub-tape, a tape-length exponent, a window-length ladder — must state beside it its REGIME-to-LENGTH RATIO: the median spread of the same quantity across DISJOINT stretches of EQUAL length, divided by its move between the longest and the shortest length. A claim whose ratio exceeds 1 is an UPPER BOUND ON PRECISION and may not be quoted as a measurement. The within spread must be COUNT-MATCHED (a random pair at every length). Scope: claims indexed by tape extent. A split-half claim is explicitly OUT of scope, because a half is one length and the clause is unsatisfiable for it without a third tape.*

  **THE DECLARED APPROXIMATION, AND ITS DIRECTION.** The census reads COMMITTED artifacts only, so a ratio published in a console log or a script comment is not counted — an omission that can only make H_NOBODY EASIER to support, and is named for that reason. The CS_STRICT test is a HEADER-NAME test: a file indexing rows by tape extent under an idiosyncratic column name is MISSED, and a file whose `rows` column means something else is OVER-counted. Both directions are live, all 148 accepted header names are dumped, and no classification in this run rests on a single file.
